# Chronicle Framework 运行日志

## 2026-11-27 确定性 provider 真机驱动 EYC 多阶段（契约 7.730）
触发：阶段①自跑闭环。用 orchestrator 起 stub（port 18770）+ 直调 AgentHostEstablishClanAutonomyEvaluation（绕过挂掉的 7890 代理所阻塞的 AgentHostPackage stage）+ renown 夹具 ChronicleW7P-manual-20261101-renown-47153。多轮真机修复 stub：分类控制误用（返回 goalClassification 而非原始 control）、bootstrap 选 enter 而非 leave、planner 选已 met 的 gold、safe-settlement 分类控制不匹配、inquiry 误选 done。最终 run `TestResults/20261127-182201-deterministic-eyc`：162 决策、3 子流程、进入真实 campaign-agent-loop，gold✓ companions✓ partySize 22/25 renown 47/50；卡在 move 空参数死循环（158 次）至 decision-limit-reached，failureCode=establish-clan-objective-routes-currently-unavailable。诊断：move 需 SettlementId，来自 mapSettlementTargets receipt 的 safe target ID，不在 decision request 中；无状态 stub 无法取得。结论：确定性 provider 已能驱动真实 Host 多阶段，但四目标完整闭环需要跨步骤记忆，记为阶段①边界。Docs 126/126/183/74/23。

## 2026-11-27 持久决策面板（契约 7.728）
触发：用户要求把决策时间线作为唯一持久前端、长期打磨为测试工具。命令：`pwsh -File harness/Run-Harness.ps1 -Phase DecisionTimeline`（无 run 参数）刷新固定 `TestResults/DecisionTimeline.html`。观察：扫描 29 个含 AgentHostProgress.jsonl 的 run，单文件 1.5MB，run 切换器与 hash 深链存在，29 run 合计 848 个模型决策；收据显示 Persistent=true、RunCount=29、TotalDecisions=848。证明：持久面板可作为唯一可切换入口，无需每次 run 换文件。未覆盖：浏览器运行时交互截图（本会话无可用浏览器 CLI），仅静态结构验证。

本文件是**每一次真实运行**（Harness 阶段、AgentHost 驱动、实机诊断、手动续跑）事实的权威来源，按时间顺序追加。
持久的技术事实、机制契约、未知、分母与验收规则在 [MAINLINE.md](MAINLINE.md)；工程契约与验收阶梯在 [DEVELOPMENT.md](DEVELOPMENT.md)。

记录要求：一次运行一段，写清触发方式（哪个阶段/哪条命令）、观察到的原版行为、失败时的根因与修复、以及留下哪些收据路径（`TestResults/<runId>`）。不在本文件复制动态任务状态。


关于收据留存：`TestResults/<runId>` 目录会被每次阶段运行末尾的 retention（`harness/Prune-TestResults.ps1`）清理，只保留最近若干个与 `DEVELOPMENT.md`、`MAINLINE.md`、`RUNLOG.md` 点名引用的运行。文档中点名的 run id 共 1,096 个，2026-11-26 盘上实际存在 148 个目录——差额主要是 `docs/Research/**` 与 `SYSTEM_INVENTORY.md` 引用的历史运行。所以本文件里的收据路径是**当时那次运行的证据指向，不保证今天还能打开**；要重跑同一观察，用同一条脚本与同一组参数（例如 `harness/Test-AgentIdlePressure.ps1 -LoadSaveName ChronicleEycDev-opening-20261126 -ChunkDays 1 -MaxChunks 1`）而不是指望旧目录。需要长期留盘的收据，必须在 `Prune-TestResults.ps1 -ReferenceDocumentPaths` 里被点名，或被写进上面三份默认文档。

## 2026-11-27 EYC 闭环推进与真实死锁边界（阶段①，未达成）

- 阶段①目标：自跑/确定性方式把 EYC 四目标从零到原生 Quest 完成。本轮用 renown 夹具 `ChronicleW7P-manual-20261101-renown-47153` 做 4 次真机（llama3.2:3b、json_schema、40–60 决策），其中一次带 `-FixtureOpponent`（注入 hostile looter party，保留主队伍）。
- 进展：gold✓（原生出售+Journal 确认）、companions✓（酒馆雇佣确认）、renown 首次真正 `completed:battle-and-captivity-resolved-and-verified`（战斗+俘虏恢复），journal renown 推到 49/50；partySize 23/25。`TestResults/20261127-eyc-closedloop-renownfixture`、`-fixture2`、`-fixture3`、`-opponent`。
- 真实死锁（不是单点 bug）：`partySize` 子流程反复 `party-capacity-blocked-until-renown`（actionsExecuted=0，原版容量锁需家族等级提升，而家族等级需 renown 50）；renown 停在 49/50，战斗胜利消耗有限可见劫匪后反复 `no-safe-action`；战斗还会打散队伍使 partySize 倒退。两个目标互为前提，形成世界状态死锁。
- HTTP 500 判定：fixture2 与 opponent 两次被 `provider-http-error`（Ollama 瞬时 500）中断。核实 `OllamaNativeProvider.ShouldRetryTransientFreeLlmApiFailure` 只对 FreeLLMAPI(31415) 的 502 重试；harness 与产品 MCM 把 `MaxHttpRequestsPerDecision` 锁为 1（成本优先，gate 06 断言 `[ValidateRange(1,2)]...=1`）。因此 loopback 500 零重试终止是**产品设计选择，不是缺陷**；改动会破坏产品成本控制契约，判定不修。
- 结论：阶段①四目标完整闭环仍未达成，剩余为真实现实死锁 + 3B 小模型 + 成本优先 provider 策略的综合边界，非可堆修补的单点问题。不再同夹具重跑（同类型失败已充分刻画）。解除条件：能提供持续 renown 来源且不打散队伍的夹具，或放宽 provider 预算契约，或更强模型。

## 2026-11-27 共享 ledger trace 节点克隆修复（契约 7.726）

- 真机 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-closedloop-renownfixture`（renown 夹具、llama3.2:3b、40 决策）跑到 `establish-clan-autonomy-unexpected-error` / `InvalidOperationException: ThrowHelper.ThrowInvalidOperationException_NodeAlreadyHasParent`，堆栈定位 `AgentHostCampaignLoop.cs:1142`。renown 路线本次真正被选中（`renown:failed:no-safe-action`），但返回时抛异常。
- 根因：该行是唯一直接挂 `ledger.Trace` live 节点的回执；EYC 父循环跨嵌套子流程复用 ledger，第一个回执被 `AdoptDelegated` 消费 trace 后，第二次挂同一节点抛异常。其它 loop 都用 `.DeepClone()`。
- 修复：`["trace"] = ledger.Trace.DeepClone()`；新增自测 `campaign-loop-reused-ledger-returns-independent-trace-clone`。build 0/0、self-test 468 场景全绿。
- 真机复核 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-closedloop-renownfixture2`（同 renown 夹具、40 决策）：`NodeAlreadyHasParent` 出现 **0 次**，异常消失；`renown` 子流程本轮首次真正 `completed:battle-and-captivity-resolved-and-verified`（阶段①四目标中 Renown 首次达成）。整体最终以 `safeSettlementRefresh:failed campaign-loop-failed` 与 `establish-clan-objective-routes-currently-unavailable` 结束（19 决策/127 动作），属另一边界，非本次 trace 修复范围。这证明该修复消除了确定性崩溃，让闭环得以推进到 Renown 完成。

## 2026-11-27 Gold 无安全动作路线封锁修复（契约 7.725）

- 根因：`TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy-opening-tuples3` 同一次真机里 gold 目标先完成一次原生出售、后以 `gold-sale-no-safe-native-action` 失败；该 termination 缺失于 `IsSafeObjectiveRouteUnavailable` 的 gold 分支，planner 因此反复重选当前不可达的 Gold 路线直到 49 决策预算耗尽。语义对称的 renown `no-safe-action` 与其余 gold 失败码均已封锁，属遗漏。
- 修复：gold 分支纳入 `gold-sale-no-safe-native-action`，当前 route fingerprint 下只封锁 Gold、planner 推进其它 EYC 目标；fingerprint 变化后自动解除。新增自测 `establish-clan-autonomy-no-safe-gold-sale-action-blocks-only-gold-route-and-allows-replan`。
- 真机复核 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy-goldblock-725`（同 opening 夹具、80 决策预算）：本次 gold 走另一条路径——原生出售完成后 → `gold-loot-no-current-visible-bandit` → partySize 中断（54 动作）→ 劫匪打断移动 → `incidentBattleSimulation` no-safe-action → 最终 `gold-sale-budget-exhausted`（80 决策全用尽）；trace 中 `gold-sale-no-safe-native-action` 出现 **0 次**。因此本次运行未走到修复路径，**不构成该修复的真机验证**；模型行为随机性使该 termination 难以按需复现。修复正确性由 467 场景自测（含新场景）、Docs 门与 tuples3 历史真机（该 termination 确会发生并令 gold 反复重选）支撑。本次运行仍属 #130 世界状态/小模型/预算综合边界的另一实例，不写为通过。FixtureUnchanged/ProcessStopped/VisualEvidence 全 true。

## 2026-11-27 招募合法三元组 schema 约束（契约 7.724）

- 真机复核：健康 opening 检查点两次本地 llama3.2:3b 跑，第一次 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy-opening-tuples` 46 决策/63 动作、gold 目标完成一次后被 Ollama HTTP500 打断；第二次 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy-opening-tuples2` 仍以 `recruitment-rejection-limit` 停止，模型继续提交 1789+slot0。根因是 `BuildDecisionSchema` 的 ownerId/slot 独立 enum 允许笛卡尔组合，catalog/prompt 的 legalTuples 不足以约束 3B 输出。
- 修复：`OllamaNativeProvider.BuildDecisionSchema` 对带 legalTuples 的 action 把 arguments 改为完整 tuple 的 `anyOf` 分支（固定 ownerId/troopId/slot/confirmPartySizeWarning 具体值），`BuildExactValueSchema` 保留数值类型与 confirm 枚举；`ProviderContractSelfTest` 新增 `provider-legal-tuples-constrain-cross-field-recruitment-choices`。
- 真机验证 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy-opening-tuples3`（健康 opening 检查点、llama3.2:3b、80 决策预算）：49 决策/77 动作，6 次招募决策全部落在合法 tuple（1789+slot1、1790+slot0、1791+slot0/1/2、1792+slot2），不再出现此前 1789+slot0 的交叉组合；`partySize` 子流程从 1 增至 7，终止原因从 `recruitment-rejection-limit` 变为 `recruitment-model-stopped-with-safe-actions`，证明 schema 层 tuple 约束生效。gold 目标完成一次原生出售（`one-native-sale-and-journal-gold-increase-verified`）。整体仍以 `gold-sale-no-safe-native-action` 结束、四目标未完成，属 #130 已记录的贫瘠世界状态+3B 小模型+预算综合边界，不再归因为招募决策面缺陷。FixtureUnchanged/ProcessStopped/VisualEvidence 全 true。

## 2026-11-27 招募合法三元组决策面修复（契约 7.723）

- 健康 opening 检查点（`ChronicleEycDev-opening-20261126`，1000 金/1 健康/士气49/粮2.0/EYC active）的 EYC 自主运行 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy-opening`（80 决策预算）跑到 27 决策/37 动作，以 `recruitment-rejection-limit` 停止。零模型核对起点状态健康；根因：模型连续 3 次提交 `recruit={ownerId:CharacterObject_1789,troopId:imperial_recruit,slot:0,confirmPartySizeWarning:0}`，但 catalog 的 ownerId=[1789,1790,1791,...] 与 slot=[1,0,0,1,2,2] 是平行数组，1789 对应的合法 slot 是 1，0 属于 1790；原生正确拒绝不存在的配对，模型因看不出配对关系反复重试到上限。
- 修复：`AgentHostEstablishClanRecruitmentLoop.BuildRecruitmentActionCatalog` 在 catalog 上发布 `legalTuples`（每项精确绑定 ownerId/troopId/slot/cost/confirmPartySizeWarning）；`EstablishClanRecruitmentSystemPrompt` 指示模型优先使用 legalTuples，不混用平行数组。自测 `establish-clan-recruitment-catalog-publishes-legal-owner-troop-slot-tuples` 通过。
- 验证：dotnet build 0 警告/0 错误；provider self-test `TestResults/_selftest-tuples.json` passed=true、465 场景、failed=0。尚未用真机重跑验证修复是否让模型完成招募——那是下一增量。

## 2026-11-27 EYC 60 决策自主闭环尝试（#130，真实边界）

- 运行：`harness/Test-AgentLiveModelLoop.ps1 -EstablishYourClanAutonomyLoop -AgentHostRuntime -LoadSaveName ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261123092350-d1a1805e -MaxProviderDecisions 60 -HostMaxElapsedSeconds 1800`，本地 llama3.2:3b + json_schema、loopback 零外部凭据，收据 `TestResults/20261127-eyc-autonomy60`。
- 结果：`Passed=false`、`establish-clan-autonomy-budget-exhausted`，60 决策/236 原生动作/153,601 输入 token，10 个目标子流程，四目标仍未完成。夹具哈希前后一致、进程停止、视觉证据通过。
- gold 目标反复失败，三种真实原因：`gold-loot-no-current-visible-bandit`（无可见劫匪可夺货）、`gold-sale-native-action-rejected`（原生拒绝当前动作且不重放）、`gold-sale-budget-exhausted-safe-map-recovered`（预算耗尽后原版 Cancel 安全回地图，Gold/时钟不变）。partySize 多次被匪徒打断或模型以安全动作停下；captivity 恢复子流程成功一次；nativeMapIncident 成功一次。
- 判读：这是 1 人 0 金贫瘠起点 + 3B 本地模型 + 60 决策预算的综合边界，不是单一可修 bug；不同失败原因分属世界状态与预算，不按同类型再跑。不把预算耗尽写成通过，也不把它写成游戏代码缺陷。#130 自然开局完整四目标闭环仍未达成，外部模型/上游凭据条件未启用。

## 2026-11-27 同起点双跑与参数层分化（契约 7.722）

- `harness/Get-AgentPathDivergence.ps1` 追加决策参数指纹（`Get-DecisionFingerprint`）：动作名路径可能收敛而参数选择分化，判据同时给 `actionShapeDiverged`（动作名序列）与 `argumentDiverged`（动作+参数序列），任一为真即 `diverged=true`。真实同起点双跑 `TestResults/20261127-divergence-towntrade-A/B`（同夹具 SHA、源提交 6628355、llama3.2:3b、2048 token、同一买入目标）：两跑都 Passed/TownTradeVerified=true，动作名序列同为 `mapSettlementTargets>move>menuSelect>tradeOptions>trade>tradeConfirm`（`actionShapeDiverged=false`），但买入商品 A=grain、B=tools，参数序列不同（`argumentDiverged=true`、整体 `diverged=true`）；判据收据 `TestResults/20261127-divergence-towntrade/DivergenceResult.json`。这是 #142 首个真实同起点分化证据：形状收敛、目标选择分化。

## 2026-11-27 同起点路径分化判据（契约 7.722）

- 新增 `harness/Get-AgentPathDivergence.ps1`（零模型）：按夹具 SHA、源提交、目标、模型、输出预算对齐起点，从 `AgentHostCampaignLoopResult.json` 提取 Native 动作路径指纹；只有共享同一可观测起点且都有完整 Host 收据才判 `diverged`，可比 run < 2 或缺收据则给 limitation 而不声称分化。

## 2026-11-27 tournament 真机收据与两处 Host 修复（契约 7.721）

- 真机入口：`ChronicleTownTradeLive-20261126` 用零模型 CLI 预置到当前 TournamentVM（town_ES3 → town_arena → town_enter_arena → ArenaMaster sign_up → confirm → `mno_tournament_event_1`），`harness/Test-AgentLiveModelLoop.ps1 -TournamentLoop` 的确定性 setup 在模型推理前完成，记录在 `TournamentSetup.json`。
- 修复一：通用 campaign-loop 在 `!MapState` 时以 `campaign-map-unavailable` 提前停止，而 Arena 锦标赛处在 `MissionState`。`AgentHostCampaignLoop` 在读状态与 parent 校验之后、通用循环之前，对精确 `tournamentProfileOnly && requestedActions.SetEquals(TournamentActions)` 直接分派到 `AgentHostTournamentLoop.RunAsync`；该独立 loop 用自己的 handler-scoped prompt，不需要地图决策。
- 修复二：`BuildActionCatalog` 原来只看 `canPlayerJoin`，但原生 `ExecuteJoinTournament` 启动比赛后 `canPlayerJoin` 仍为 true、`isCurrentMatchActive=true`，导致 catalog 永远给 `tournamentJoin`，模型连续 24 次 Join 死循环到 `tournament-budget-exhausted-left-native-screen`。改为 `!isCurrentMatchActive && canPlayerJoin` 才给 Join，比赛激活后只给 Skip/Watch/Leave；`TournamentSystemPrompt` 同步写明激活后走 skip/watch。回归自测 `tournament-active-match-skips-even-when-can-join-remains-true` 覆盖。
- 真机回执 `TestResults/20261127-tournament-live-catalogfix`：本地 Ollama llama3.2:3b + json_schema，`Passed=true`、`TournamentVerified=true`、`AcceptanceSlice=Real-model Native Arena tournament observation to a resolved result with no unverified victory or reward claim`、`HostTerminationReason=native-tournament-result-observed-without-player-victory-or-reward-claim`。7 个原版 handler、0 外来动作，Native 解析出 NPC 胜者 lord_1_48/约戎、playerWon=false、`playerVictoryVerified=false`、`rewardVerified=false`、`renownVerified=false`，Campaign 日/Stop 时钟不变，夹具 hash 前后一致、进程停止、视觉证据通过。这是 #141 首个真实本地模型 tournament 观察回执，不证明玩家胜利或奖金。

## 2026-11-26 tournament 分类入口与 live-model 观察切片（契约 7.721）

## 2026-11-21 … 2026-11-22 开发与 Harness 契约沿革（原 DEVELOPMENT.md 时间条目）

以下条目原在 `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md` 顶部，与契约版本段落混在一起。它们记录的是每次真实运行与诊断，故整体移到本文件，逐字保留（含引用的 `TestResults/` 收据）。契约层面的稳定结论留在 DEVELOPMENT 的 `Contract 7.<n>` 段落里。

2026-11-22 FreeLLMAPI cost guard: local read-only DB review of the active `noema-free` profile found 11 enabled routes: six custom providers without recorded price/quota, plus free-tier Kilo/OVH/Google entries; one Google route also records non-zero paid rates. Direct Kilo/OVH free entries were absent from `/v1/models?available=true`; Google Flash-Lite returned 429 with a long Retry-After. `/api/fallback` and `/api/settings` require a Dashboard credential (unified API key receives 401), so the active chain cannot be verified through the authenticated management API. Harness contract 7.654 now classifies `auto:*`, `fusion*`, and `free-router` as multi-route; actual model inference through them is rejected before game launch or smoke inference, while zero-call health inspection remains available. Direct FreeLLMAPI models still require an available listing and a bounded smoke before a Campaign test. DSH XD Pool campaign preflight is a separate explicit direct-`:free` loopback path: it verifies the exact model ID via authenticated `/v1/models`, never reads or enables the plugin's combo chain, and performs no inference itself.

2026-11-22 provider error evidence: a real Campaign response with a single `choices` entry but no object-valued `message` previously raised `provider-content-missing` without preserving HTTP status, route, finish reason, or reported usage. This made an observed Host receipt of `provider-content-missing` with every provider diagnostic null impossible to distinguish from other malformed-success shapes. Contract 7.655 now records only bounded metadata (status, selected route, response shape, model, finish reason, usage counts, and safe response headers) for that branch; it still discards the response body and never records credentials or free-text content. AgentHostPackage has a synthetic regression for the malformed choice and must pass before another real request.

2026-11-22 Native 1-vs-11 battle handoff correction: user-confirmed solo MainHero combat was reproduced in `TestResults/20261122-021134` with one MainHero versus 11 Native looters. The original game advanced Deployment→Battle before the Host first Mission-status read; the Host issued no Auto Deploy/Ready/F6, handed MainHero to Native AI, returned through Native aftermath, used 0 model calls, preserved the source hash and removed the verified disposable clone. A separate earlier troop-bearing probe `TestResults/20261122-005344` showed callback-unbound Deployment can persist, so the Host now distinguishes `playerTeam.nonMainHeroAgentCount`: when it is zero, it waits for Native transition instead of applying troop deployment to an empty side. AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261122-023134` verifies both the ordinary troop-bearing AutoDeploy/Ready path and the solo Native-wait path. The exact live case where Host first reads the 1-vs-11 Mission while it is still in Deployment has not been observed; that timing branch remains synthetic-only. No live model call or persistent Campaign change was made.

2026-11-21 Persistent EYC budget-boundary repair: the 4-decision real-model run `TestResults/20261121-214516` entered Native Town Trade but stopped before the model read offers; Native `inventoryScreen.mode` was `Trade`, while the 1.4.8 receipt omitted `hasPendingChanges`, so the previous safety gate refused cancellation and the test could not checkpoint. `CanSafelyCancelUntouchedNativeTrade` now also accepts the exact immediate Native Town `menuSelect(trade)` receipt when there has been no trade/commit action; explicit `hasPendingChanges=true`, any sale action, a different Town option or a different UI remain rejected. `BuildReceipt` retains the menu/option pair for that guard. The exact missing-field path is covered by `AgentHostPackage` `TestResults/20261121-221956`; zero-model-only test.

2026-11-21相机跟随复测与对照纠正：旧跑次`TestResults/20261121-203620`并未得到有效的玩家视角对照——主角仍由玩家控制的9个样本中，正常水平视角样本为0，镜头在测试夹具里回到俯视；该组标记为无效，不再称作“F6镜头基准”。这组真实AI镜头样本同时测得相机方位与主角朝向的最大偏差约3.076弧度，符合“画面转到角色侧面”的方向性问题。当前Native相机更新后的窄修正只在偏差超过0.02弧度时让镜头朝向跟随MainHero；`TestResults/20261121-205353`编译及Native handoff probe通过，13个AI样本中的最大朝向偏差为0.0173弧度，画面关键帧显示人物背后和地平线。该run为零模型调用、正式源档hash未变、临时档/游戏进程清理完毕，30秒视频成功提取24张索引关键帧后删除。 `TestResults/20261121-210612`生产Host战斗路径也通过：0模型调用、19条Native命令、达到原版战后状态、正式源档hash未变、临时存档及游戏进程已清理、桌面输入隔离通过；30秒/10fps视频收录AI接管后的6秒内24张、间隔0.25秒的关键帧并在索引后删除MP4。画面持续为人物后方第三人称并可见地平线，但有些帧出现运动模糊；截图还不能区分这是相机抖动还是主角/地形运动造成的画面变化。上下抖动仍未判定：13个稀疏样本的镜头俯仰为约-0.66至0弧度，相机相对主角Z偏移范围约0.962米；Native内部shake触发标记在这些样本中未激活，但这种低频取样不足以排除每秒多次的视觉抖动，且相对高度变化也可能来自地形/骑乘移动。后续只需对AI相机段作短时高帧率聚焦记录；在完成该验证前，不宣称抖动已解决或归因于原生shake。

2026-11-21 battle Host probe and Mission-end race: `TestResults/20261121-100311` showed the deployment screen reports zero formations before Battle, then the Host correctly found and delegated one live formation. The probe's expected-action list had incorrectly used the pre-battle Deployment count; it now uses the Host's post-transition result and checks `delegationIssued == (delegatedFormationCount > 0)`. The next run `TestResults/20261121-101555` exposed a separate Native race: the Host read an active Mission state, then the Mission ended before the detailed battle-status read. The status call returned `mission ended`, and the Host marked AI control lost even though all 65 active Battle samples showed MainHero AI-controlled with the Mission input controller inactive. The Host now re-reads Native state after an unsupported/stale battle-status read; an active Mission remains a control-loss signal, while an ended/returned Mission is normal completion. Package regression `TestResults/20261121-102935` and production no-model battle run `TestResults/20261121-103219` pass. That production run completed MainHero autopilot and Native aftermath, preserved the source save hash, removed the disposable save, stopped the game, and passed desktop input isolation. Its 58 active production Host status samples show pitch `-0.259` to `-0.224` radians with MainHero AI-controlled; the separate retained engine frame `TestResults/20261121-063339/Temp/TimelineFrames/native-ai-battle-camera-sustained.bmp` confirms this target renders as normal third-person with the horizon visible. The Native `OrderOfBattleVM.ExecuteBeginMission` path still reports a `NullReferenceException` after deployment has successfully transitioned to Battle; this run completed despite that recorded exception, so it remains a separate Native-callback issue. Contract version: 7.622.

2026-11-21 camera correction, second pass: the earlier report that `TestResults/20261121-063339/Temp/TimelineFrames/native-ai-battle-camera-sustained.bmp` showed a behind-the-hero view was incorrect; the captured frame shows MainHero's face. Level pitch and visible horizon alone did not satisfy the behind-camera requirement. Source review separated Native MainAgent camera bearing from its spectator-follow bearing: adding PI applies to the spectator camera, not the player camera. Chronicle's AI battle lease now tracks `MainAgent.LookDirectionAsAngle` with a bounded 20-radian/second turn rate and small dead zone, holds the selected third-person pitch, and smooths camera height relative to the Agent's physical position to remove animation bob. It leaves first-person mode unchanged and restores the user's original third-person bearing/pitch when the AI lease ends. This remains a view-only change and does not alter Native orders, battle outcome or Campaign time. `TestResults/20261121-181529` passed the no-model Native handoff probe: 13 AI-controlled samples, maximum camera/facing error 0.0178 radians, camera/Agent vertical-offset range 0.0133 meters, stable third-person mode, and an engine frame showing MainHero from behind with the horizon visible. `TestResults/20261121-181942` passed the exact production AgentHost Native autopilot path and retained 30 seconds of explicit-window capture at 10 fps; 24 time-indexed keyframes were extracted, the MP4 was removed after extraction, and the contact sheet shows the same behind-the-hero view during the Host lease. The run made zero model calls, kept the source save hash unchanged, removed the disposable save, stopped the game, and passed desktop-input isolation. Earlier `TestResults/20261121-062745` remains valid evidence that `-1.3659` is a near-vertical view; the `063339` image remains valid as a frame, but its former “behind view” interpretation is superseded. Contract version: 7.634.

2026-11-21 stale-target checkpoint probe `TestResults/20261121-150121`: resumed checkpoint `67cb6710` while the Native MainParty still showed a stopped move target `town_EW4`. The Host's read-only current `mapSettlementTargets` receipt no longer contained that destination, so it stopped before replaying the hidden target or issuing any Native action. One model classification response was used (1,216 prompt / 168 completion tokens); provider cost is unknown. The new safe checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261121150929-3c50ee87` (SHA-256 `B770246F5A0F8E8CEA8EE525F254864BB5ECC619955BF930E623543B6FB356F8`) preserves the same Campaign/map state. This exposed a Harness continuation defect: MapState alone was considered a safe checkpoint even when a saved in-flight target cannot be observed after reload. The Host treats a missing saved destination as temporary route loss: it blocks the integrated objectives at that Native state and enters the existing safe-settlement refresh, which asks the model to choose from current visible targets. The target is never replayed unless it reappears, and no clock mode is changed. AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261121-152021` passes the target/day gate. Live run `TestResults/20261121-155519` showed the safe refresh child still returned a stopped move to its parent before querying targets. CampaignMapLoop now bypasses that handoff only for `safe-settlement-arrival`, leaving ordinary campaign recovery unchanged; AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261121-162157` passes Build/package regressions including the scoped stall bypass with zero model/Native calls; live safe-refresh execution remains to be verified. Docs exposed missing UTF-8 BOMs after PowerShell edits, so Check-Docs.ps1 and Run-Harness.ps1 now retain BOMs for non-ASCII source. Contract version: 7.633.

2026-11-21 ordered decision-batch handling: The live Dots encounter response included 10 tool calls, followed by 2 and 3 calls on correction, while Native offered a single `menuSelect` action. Rejection was safe but left the incident battle unfinished and prevented a new checkpoint. The provider adapter now validates the first ordered tool call against the exact current decision schema and discards later calls from that same response. The Host still sends only one Native action and reobserves before the next model decision; it never skips an invalid first call in favor of a later one. Product-run and Harness logs record only the number discarded, not tool arguments. Build/AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261121-075400` passes the one-call, trailing-call discard, and invalid-first-call guards with zero real model calls and zero Native gameplay commands. Contract version: 7.620. A live FreeLLMAPI verification is still required.

2026-11-21 Native battle decision batching correction: the real-model run `TestResults/20261121-073053` resumed same Campaign `79b4b1da`, pursued `looters_1671`, then reached the Native encounter choice menu (`attack`, `surrender`, `leave`). FreeLLMAPI Dots returned 10, then 2 and 3 tool calls for that one `menuSelect` decision; Host rejected each response and sent no Native action from those responses. After one correction per response the battle child failed, leaving the encounter open and preventing a verified checkpoint. This is a provider/Host protocol mismatch with a model that emits a batch despite `parallel_tool_calls=false`; the tool count and UI evidence do not reveal the discarded calls' arguments. The Host now treats the returned array as an ordered proposal, validates the first call against the exact current action catalog, executes at most that one, discards all later calls, and reobserves before another decision. If the first call is invalid, the whole response is rejected; the Host never skips to a later call. Accepted runtime telemetry records only how many later calls were discarded, not their contents. Regression package `TestResults/20261121-075400` passed Build/AgentHostPackage with zero live-model calls and zero Native gameplay commands. Next bounded live continuation starts from the still-hash-verified `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261121071011-79b4b1da`; the failed `10b46df9` copy had no verified hash and is not selected. EYC remains incomplete; the new provider normalization has only synthetic package evidence until its first live run.

2026-11-18 failed-provider usage accounting: the bounded FreeLLMAPI `auto:reliable` continuation (`TestResults/20261118-083314`) received a required-tool-call-missing response from `dots-studio/dots-3-note-preview:free`; the Host rejected it before any Native action. Its sanitized failure event contained 1437 prompt and 256 completion tokens, but the persistent EYC receipt and manifest counted 0 because they aggregated only accepted `provider-decision` rows. The progress log retained the failed response usage; the persistent aggregator now includes failed-response token counts separately from accepted decisions, exposes provider failure counts, and marks cost estimates incomplete when a response has no price estimate. A migration now safely adds new accounting fields to the existing v1 manifest and reconciles a matching failed-usage receipt by Campaign ID and checkpoint name/hash, so the existing request is counted once while preserving old totals as lower bounds. A no-model self-test covers failed-response tokens, unknown cost, and migration of a manifest missing the new fields; the Agent Host runtime-log self-test also verifies MCM summaries. The bounded continuation `TestResults/20261118-163334` reached FreeLLMAPI but timed out before a response and sent no Native action. Its proxy log contains a time-correlated `google/gemini-3.5-flash-lite` forbidden/403 failure 17.077 seconds after request start; because the Host received no provider request ID this is not a unique correlation. The current catalog marks the Google route exhausted; direct connectivity checks through the shared 7890 proxy succeeded for Google, so the evidence does not show a general proxy outage. In `TestResults/20261118-171020` the persistent manifest now records 789 requests/786 accepted decisions/3 failures, 4219/1280 known failed tokens, and incomplete token/cost coverage; a safe same-Campaign checkpoint was saved and all game processes stopped.
- 2026-11-18 compact EYC prompts and provider recovery: the exact EYC classifier succeeded on Dots (936/297 tokens), but the first Companion response returned two required tool calls; Host rejected both. Companion prompts are now projected to the current Native Map/menu/Mission/dialogue stage (725–935 characters vs the former 4039-character fixed prompt), with no-model contracts for all four states. Failure traces now record prompt/user/observation byte counts, current action/control names and feedback kind without their content. `AgentHostPackage` and Docs passed before the live recovery test. In `TestResults/20261118-190839`, the Host received two calls despite `parallel_tool_calls=false`, rejected them before Native execution, then used the one allowed correction; the retry returned one `mapSettlementTargets` action. Dots then selected/moved to EW6, entered Town backstreet and Tavern, navigated/interacted with a Wanderer, and progressed through five visible dialogue responses. It declined the unaffordable offer, returned to Town backstreet, then submitted another `town_tavern` request; the final captured Native state remained at Backstreet, so successful re-entry was not verified. The run used 16 provider requests (one failed multi-call, 15 valid decisions), 50,980 prompt/4,464 completion tokens, 51 Native actions, and no verified EYC Journal progress. A safe same-Campaign checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261118191118-e4cc3e8c` (SHA-256 `A7DDD45DA28EF3D2400DDC0640C7597C9D0BA4574D33E7EB7A2BED667C81CE50`) was saved at Town backstreet; game/Host processes are clean. FreeLLMAPI reports no cost estimate, so cumulative cost remains incomplete. Continue from this checkpoint with up to 20 provider decisions and preserve rejection recovery; do not replay from the older Map checkpoint. EYC remains incomplete.

2026-11-18 sustained Native Battle camera verification: the final scoped Harmony postfix passed in TestResults/20261118-081146. The starting pitch was the near-vertical -1.3659; handoff selected -0.2, then all 28 AI-controlled samples stayed between -0.2345 and -0.2000. The sustained frame TestResults/20261118-081146/Temp/TimelineFrames/native-ai-battle-camera-sustained.bmp shows MainHero from behind with the horizon visible, rather than the overhead shot in earlier captures. The patch receipt confirms installation. No model call was made, the Campaign clock remained stopped, the source save hash was unchanged, the disposable clone was removed, and the game process stopped.

2026-11-18 AI battle camera correction: a 1.4.8 no-model Native battle probe (`TestResults/20261118-065844/Temp/BattleMissionNativeAiHandoffProbe.json`) measured the third-person camera at its near-vertical Native clamp (`elevation=-1.3659`, `firstPerson=false`) before and throughout the MainHero AI lease. The test window is intentionally non-activating, so this does not prove that the AI handoff caused the initial angle; it does establish the abnormal camera state and that the current AI handoff left it unchanged. Native `MissionScreen` initializes third-person elevation at `-0.2`. On Chronicle's MainHero AI battle handoff, the mod now restores that Native shallow angle only when the camera is near-vertical (below -0.7 radians), synchronizes the controller's `CustomLookDir` so Native `MissionScreen` does not reapply the previous vertical angle, and clears any stale vertical camera delta; it preserves other player camera angles and first/third-person mode, then restores the saved look direction when AI control ends. The opt-in no-model probe follows the original Native Auto Deploy/Ready chain, captures a real engine frame after handoff, and checks camera angle across the lease; it uses only a disposable clone and no model. This is a view-only correction; it does not change combat orders, AI behavior, or Campaign time. The first post-fix runtime attempt (`TestResults/20261118-070936`) stopped before Bannerlord launched because Steam's latest connection state was Logged Off; no post-fix visual result is claimed. A later camera test (`TestResults/20261118-072933`) confirmed the first angle reset appeared in an engine frame but Native `MissionScreen` reapplied the old look direction on subsequent frames; the final correction now synchronizes that direction, and the probe must be rerun before claiming camera verification.

2026-11-18 sustained camera correction follow-up: `TestResults/20261118-074523` showed that a one-time camera pitch and `CustomLookDir` update still returned to Native's `-1.3659` clamp on later `MissionScreen.UpdateCamera` ticks. The mod now installs a postfix on that Native update, but only changes the camera during Chronicle's active MainHero AI field-battle lease. If the third-person angle again crosses the near-vertical threshold, the postfix restores the current saved valid angle (or `-0.2` when the previous angle was near-vertical) and rebuilds that rendered frame's rotation from the current Native bearing. The probe asserts that the patch installed, captures an engine frame, and samples the camera throughout the lease. This patch has passed compilation but still needs the new runtime probe to prove sustained visual behavior.

2026-11-18 Steam reconnect diagnosis: the Steam `connection_log.txt` latest state was Logged Off after `SteamServerConnectFailure_t` results `Service Unavailable` and `Busy`, with automatic reconnect delays of 367 and 525 seconds. `TestResults/20261118-070936` therefore stopped before Bannerlord launch; this is Steam's recorded connection state, not a Bannerlord crash and not an inference about the user's VPN. `Ensure-ChronicleSteamClientReady` now parses the sanitized failure category and reconnect delay, then fails immediately when stable Steam processes report a reconnect delay longer than the Harness timeout. It does not change Steam settings, authenticate, launch Bannerlord, or bypass the readiness gate. A regression covers the Busy/525-second log shape. This avoids spending 45 seconds on a retry that cannot occur within the bounded test window; when Steam reconnects inside the timeout the normal readiness poll still proceeds.

2026-11-18 desktop cursor isolation follow-up: `TestResults/20261118-043434` stopped before inference because `GetClipCursor` reported a 3×3 region while the game was visible and non-foreground; no crash or model call occurred. The Harness now snapshots the original desktop clip. It may restore to the current full virtual-desktop bounds only when that original snapshot was unrestricted, the same foreground owner remains, and the game is not foreground; pre-existing restrictions are left untouched. It never calls `ClipCursor(NULL)`, activates the game, or injects mouse/keyboard input. `TestResults/20261118-044532` Smoke then passed: the game remained non-foreground/non-activating, muted, and cursor-unrestricted; no restoration call was needed, so the restoration branch is covered by deterministic policy tests but not yet by a live clip-changing run. This replaces the previous generic crash/mouse-block diagnosis; cursor clipping was the actual observed gate.

2026-11-18 Persistent EYC startup duplication removed: `TestResults/20261118-060315` proves the standalone Smoke passed and the later live child failed before game launch because Steam reported `user-not-logged-on`; no model request or Native action occurred and no persistent checkpoint was overwritten. The live child independently verifies its own Bannerlord/Host launch, shader dialog, non-activating window, cursor state, input lock and cleanup. The PersistentEycCampaign phase therefore no longer launches a separate Smoke client between Build and package checks; standalone Smoke remains available for module/UI diagnosis.

2026-11-18 FreeLLMAPI output-effort diagnosis: `free-router` successfully classified the active EYC goal and issued native map/party/encounter steps, but later decisions returned plain text at the exact 1024- and 2048-token response limits (`finish_reason=length`, zero tool calls); a separate JSON-object-mode request returned HTTP 429 before any decision. No raw model body or credential was logged. Current FreeLLMAPI metadata advertises both `reasoning_effort` and tool calling for the free router; its maintained source normalizes and forwards that effort separately, and the Kilo adapter policy drops `response_format` but not `reasoning_effort` ([sampling parameter contract](https://github.com/tashfeenahmed/freellmapi/blob/main/server/src/lib/sampling-params.ts), [provider registry/policy](https://github.com/tashfeenahmed/freellmapi/blob/main/server/src/providers/index.ts)). Host now sends a configured non-`none` effort such as `low` for FreeLLMAPI only when the selected model advertises support, even if the separate thinking flag is disabled, and records what was applied. A real `reasoning_effort=low` continuation still ended at the 1024-token limit, so the next Host change dynamically narrows the Battle/captivity prompt to the current Native action scope instead of repeating postbattle rules during pursuit/encounter choices. The next bounded live attempt stays on the free router, required tool calls, one request per decision and a 1024-token ceiling; no further token-cap increase or local/paid model call is planned for this experiment.

2026-11-18 Native captivity escape handoff: the user confirmed that after MainHero escapes captivity, the visible Native “Continue…” option returns the Hero to free Campaign Map near the captors. The prior real EYC receipt recorded `MapState/menu_captivity_end_wilderness_escape` after BattleSimulation, but the battle adapter did not expose that release-page action and the next provider response lacked a tool call. Host now shares the source-verified Native release-menu predicate/action builder with the captivity loop; BattleSimulation exposes only the currently enabled `mno_continue` when captivity recovery is explicitly enabled and verifies the current menu/option again before submission. `TestResults/20261118-042138` AgentHostPackage passed the focused Fake Native/Provider regression plus package checks with no model call, Native game command, or client launch; this does not claim a live post-escape replay or determine restored Party size/time mode.

2026-11-18 model-route coverage: a real EYC continuation using FreeLLMAPI's Kilo Dots free route completed 4 structured decisions and 3 Native actions, then returned no tool call with `finish_reason=content_filter` on the battle-related decision; a second same-route response was truncated at the 1024-token cap. A real Nemotron Kilo-free route returned 15/16 tool calls in one response even after the Harness sent `parallel_tool_calls=false`; Host correctly dispatched no action. Gemma 4 31B IT advertised JSON Schema but returned HTTP 429; FreeLLMAPI's `auto:noema-free` reached OVH/Mistral, returned two decisions, then 429; `free-router` reached AI Horde/Koboldcpp/Gemma Heretic and returned HTTP 502. In each failure, the previous checkpoint was preserved or a new same-Campaign checkpoint was hash-verified; no EYC target progress occurred and FreeLLMAPI provided no billable usage estimate. The Host sends `parallel_tool_calls=false` only when that capability is declared and still rejects multiple decisions. The EYC Harness now has an explicit per-decision HTTP retry cap (default 1, selectable 1–2) so one upstream fallback can be tried only when requested; retry budget does not increase model decision count or Native-action allowance. This is now covered by Build/AgentHostPackage; the next live `free-router` run will use cap 2 to verify whether FreeLLMAPI can route around the first upstream 502. The model selector accepts one explicit ID and verifies it against the current configured provider catalog instead of a stale hard-coded list. Contract version 7.591.

2026-11-17 desktop input isolation: a live external Host bypassed the in-mod startup path and entered a 3D Mission without the input-owner lock; the user observed Bannerlord capture the mouse. Live Harness and Native battle-probe tests now arm that existing input lock in the game process and require its diagnostic receipt before model/battle actions; Mission probes additionally require the empty Native Mission input context. Harness starts Bannerlord hidden, then applies `WS_EX_NOACTIVATE` and shows it with `SWP_NOACTIVATE | SWP_NOZORDER`; it can find the hidden main window to set these styles before display and fails the receipt if Bannerlord is foreground. Smoke `TestResults/20261118-001042` passed: game PID 24796 remained nonactivating/nonforeground (foreground PID 33192), the Agent input lock was observed, and `GetClipCursor` covered the full virtual desktop. The focused no-model 3D test `TestResults/20261118-002009` passed at `MissionState/Deployment` (`battle_terrain_003`): PID 37432 was not foreground, `GetClipCursor` covered the virtual desktop (0,0–1707,960), and logs confirmed both Agent ownership and empty Mission input. It made zero model calls; source save remained unchanged, the disposable copy was removed, and game processes stopped. A broader formation probe reached the same Mission and passed its desktop-isolation gate but later failed at the separate Native `missionFinishDeployment` readiness check; it is not counted as a full battle-probe pass. Product MCM/F10 behavior is unchanged. Contract version 7.588.

2026-11-17 Persistent-startup crash capture: the failed continuation `TestResults/20261116-173247` made zero model decisions and no Native map action; its fresh client never reached the Campaign-ready marker. Windows Application Error/Windows Error Reporting records `Bannerlord.BLSE.Standalone.exe` APPCRASH with managed exception code `0xe0434352`, but the retained event has no managed exception message or stack, so the crashing module is not identified yet. The startup loop previously inspected RGL exception text but did not treat a `Bannerlord Crash Report` window title as terminal, allowing repeated readiness polling. `Agent-Cli.ps1` now snapshots the process-owned crash window, PID-correlated Windows Application Error/WER events (1000/1001/1026), and RGL/watchdog tails, stops only the test-owned process tree, restores its temporary engine config when cleanup succeeds, and exits before any model request; it does not click the crash-report window. Deterministic Docs checks cover title matching, event correlation/redaction boundaries, and reject ordinary game/warning titles. This improves capture/cleanup and does not claim to fix the underlying runtime crash. The separate pursuit test is only valid when its Native receipt proves the selected `targetPartyId` equals the intended bandit; a coordinate-only move does not satisfy that condition. Contract version 7.585.

2026-11-17 crash-log correlation: the local Steam manifest lists public build 26566465 (SteamDB labels it v1.5.7), while the target branch v1.4.8 is build 24573425. The `default20261116.log` line “for game v1.5.1” describes MBOptionScreen's selected compatibility implementation, not a verified full Native runtime version. The same log shows mismatched compatibility hooks and a `MissingMethodException` inside the BUTR CrashReport path. WER dump/archive files have expired, so the initiating exception remains unknown; do not label these visible errors as the crash cause without the initiating stack. The failed run performed no target selection and is invalid evidence for the v1.4.8 gameplay contract. The user reconfirmed v1.4.8 as target. Harness Preflight now stops before Build/launch unless Steam's selected and mounted branches both show `v1.4.8`; Docs `TestResults/20261117-194803` passed the synthetic branch cases and an actual-manifest probe correctly blocked selected=v1.4.8 / mounted=public / build=26566465. Steam is still applying the branch. Contract version 7.585.
2026-11-17 1.4.8 Native Smoke: Steam selected+mounted build 24573425 / Native changeset 119303. Preflight `TestResults/20261117-203122` and Build+AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261117-203245` passed. The default 45s cold Smoke stopped at 48.76s while Native XML loading was still active, with no RGL crash marker; one repeat at 180s passed `TestResults/20261117-203938` after StartupReadySeconds=64.36, with Chronicle lifecycle and MCM/key registration visible and no crash. 2026-11-11 MainHero controller safety probe (Research only, #132): the user confirmed that Native F6 delegates troop formations but MainHero still does not act autonomously. Added an opt-in `BattleMissionControllerProbe` Harness phase that uses a disposable Campaign clone and zero model calls; its Harness-only Native command temporarily sets `Agent.Controller=AI` for at most 10 seconds, records MainAgent/formation/health/position and Campaign clock state, and restores Player control on explicit cleanup, timeout, Mission change, or stop. This command is not in the AgentHost model action catalog, and no product capability is approved by this probe. `Run-Harness.ps1 -Phase Build` passed at `TestResults/20261111-092037`; Native probe `TestResults/20261111-092652` passed with 0 model calls, unchanged MainHero position/HP during AI control, successful automatic Player restoration, unchanged source hash, and disposable-clone cleanup. `AgentHostPackage` passed at `TestResults/20261111-093535`; the probe remains research evidence, not a shipped MainHero-autonomy feature.

All operations remain Harness-only, disposable, and zero-model; the refreshed-order runtime sample is pending.

2026-11-11 Added a separate zero-model opt-in `BattleMissionRtsCameraProbe` to test the installed-source RTSCamera handoff pattern while keeping MainHero as Mission.MainAgent: Native AI controller, Alarmed WatchState, clear the current private player-troop-in-formation flag, and rebind the current Move order. The probe restores these fields within the disposable Mission; no AgentHost action or product behavior is added. `TestResults/20261111-105107` passed as a test protocol: the RTSCamera-pattern subset restored Controller, WatchState and Formation flag, but the MainHero position stayed fixed and HP fell 78→8 before Native `prisoner_wait`/Party20→0. It is negative movement evidence, not a safe-combat feature; no model calls and the source save remained unchanged.

2026-11-11 Added `BattleMissionMovementInputProbe` as a separate opt-in, no-model check of one 0.5-second retreat-relative `MovementInputVector` after Native `MissionMainAgentController.ControlTick` during a field Battle Mission. A Harness-only lease enables the postfix for the same disposable test Mission and clears the vector on expiry/mission change/stop; production `missionInput` remains restricted to friendly Location Missions, and AgentHost receives no new action. `TestResults/20261111-113445` passed with 0 model calls: position changed 0.226 units during the bounded input, Campaign time remained Stop/day-stable, the source save stayed hash-identical, the disposable clone was removed, and the game process exited. This confirms only a short movement pulse, not autonomous battle navigation or safety.

2026-11-11 Added an opt-in `BattleMissionAgentNavigatorProbe` to test the installed Native `SandBox.AgentNavigator` on a retreat point away from the nearest enemy, for at most 8 seconds while Campaign time stays stopped. The Harness-only action must restore Player control on arrival, timeout, mission change/end, cancel or stop; it is not in AgentHost. Native result is pending.

2026-11-11 First `BattleMissionAgentNavigatorProbe` run `TestResults/20261111-120254` was not valid movement evidence: the lease validator reused the Location-only guard, which required MainAgent.Controller=Player and immediately cleared the test's AI-controller lease. The code now has a separate guard requiring the same active Campaign field Battle Mission with its probe owner AI-controlled; the new guarded rerun will be the movement result. No Native test result is inferred from the first run.

2026-11-07 OpenSpec 文档治理接入：安装并校验官方独立版 OpenSpec CLI 1.16.0（SHA256与官方发布清单一致）。`Get-ContextPack.ps1`现在会附带唯一活动OpenSpec change及任务/Artifact状态；`Run-Harness.ps1 -Phase Docs`通过`Check-Docs.ps1`执行`openspec validate --all`，拒绝超过一个活动change，并验证CLI解析到当前仓库。OpenSpec生成的技能和变更Markdown由CLI治理，不塞入内容文档路由；GitHub Project仍独占产品顺序/状态，OpenSpec仅记录唯一产品Issue下的一个相干变更，Git记录代码历史，TestResults保存完整收据。`TestResults/20261107-064423` Docs通过（OpenSpec 1.16.0，1个活动change，1/1有效），`20261107-064923` AgentHostPackage通过（361 Provider、26 CampaignLoop、26 BattleSimulationLoop场景，0真实模型调用）。这让新/压缩上下文自动看到当前变更状态，避免只靠重复口头重建进度；它不替代运行证据或GitHub板。

2026-11-02 Native map-conversation movement interrupt: `AgentHostNativeMapMovementMonitor.IsActiveMapMove` now ends a passive travel-observation window as soon as the current MapState includes an active Native `context.conversation` object. The fresh state returns to the calling Agent loop to handle its visible no-choice Continue or clickable answers; the monitor issues no clock action. A Fake Native regression opens a map conversation on its first monitored poll and proves the wait exits before its four-second deadline, without Pause. This change is source/contract-level only; natural map-conversation interruption during a live model travel wait remains unverified.

2026-11-02 Training Issue route map-dialogue recovery: the optional EYC training-issue route now handles a Native non-encounter MapState conversation that interrupts travel. It exposes only current clickable answers, or Continue for a current text-only line; direct issue acceptance remains limited to the contacted training giver and the source-identified response. An unrelated conversation cannot inherit the Issue giver's acceptance catalog. `TestResults/20261102-111321` passed the initial package regression; `TestResults/20261102-164412` adds a Fake Native/Provider full-loop case proving the route sends exactly one current safe reply, hides the unrelated Issue acceptance and `observeAgain`, then yields a newly appeared encounter to the parent without clock actions. The package passes with 314 Provider, 24 CampaignLoop and 22 BattleSimulation scenarios, zero real-model calls and zero Native gameplay commands. This is packaged Host evidence, not a natural StoryMode or model-run result.

2026-11-02 FreeLLMAPI empty structured-reply diagnostic: a synthetic HTTP-200 JSON-Schema response with `message.content=null` and one `tool_calls` entry remains a fail-closed `provider-content-missing` result. The diagnostic records only response-shape fields (content kind, tool-call count, finish reason and selected format); the self-test includes a private marker to verify response arguments and IDs are not copied into the exception. `TestResults/20261102-172114` AgentHostPackage passed with 315 Provider, 24 CampaignLoop and 22 BattleSimulation scenarios, zero real-model calls and zero Native commands. This tests the local parser against a synthetic envelope; the earlier live FreeLLMAPI content-missing response shape remains unknown and needs a permitted natural model rerun.

2026-11-02 FreeLLMAPI no-choice route diagnostic: a synthetic HTTP-200 response with `choices=[]` now includes the observed choice count, error-field presence, configured response mode, sanitized response model ID and sanitized `X-Routed-Via` in the bounded `provider-content-missing` failure. The test includes a private upstream-body marker and verifies neither it nor any model output is copied into the error. `TestResults/20261102-182210` AgentHostPackage passed; this synthetic envelope is consistent with the older generic “no structured message” receipt but does not prove the actual historical upstream response had zero choices. No model inference or Native command was issued.

2026-11-03 DeepSeek provider routing: the production Host now resolves explicit HTTP(S)/ALL proxy settings first and, when no proxy is configured, uses the already-running `127.0.0.1:7890` listener for remote HTTPS while keeping loopback services direct and respecting `NO_PROXY`. The game child-process launcher passes the selected proxy without changing user/machine settings; health and smoke receipts identify only the proxy source. Removed the Harness's separate PowerShell `/models` preflight so health and inference both use the same production Host transport. `AgentHostPackage` passed 318 provider scenarios, 24 Campaign-loop and 22 battle-loop scenarios with 0 model calls/0 Native commands (`TestResults/20261103-022521`); the read-only DeepSeek Flash health check passed with 2 catalog rows and 0 model calls/0 Native commands (`TestResults/20261103-022727`); the one-call real DeepSeek Flash smoke passed required-tool-call classification at `TestResults/20261103-022920` (780 prompt / 64 completion tokens, 384 cache hits, estimated ¥0.001583232, 0 Native commands). That first smoke inherited a 256-token output cap; the smoke Harness now pins only its process-local cap to 128, without changing production/user settings. The corrected second smoke passed at `TestResults/20261103-023238` (780 prompt / 64 completion tokens, 640 cache hits, estimated ¥0.00098112, 128-token cap, 0 Native commands). Contract version 7.498.

The game child-process launcher passes the selected proxy without changing user/machine settings; health and smoke receipts identify only the proxy source. 2026-11-02 GitHub CLI network diagnosis: GitHub REST/web reads and the connected GitHub issue reader succeeded while `gh project item-list` intermittently returned GraphQL EOF. The local `127.0.0.1:7890` listener and Git proxy setting were present; direct TLS failed, but a retry of the same GraphQL query succeeded, and a Project status filter returned the sole In Progress item #130. Explicit per-process proxy variables did not resolve the EOF. GitHub read-only Git refs worked, but Schannel TLS handshakes during `git push` failed after the proxy returned `CONNECT 200`. Switching the Git TLS backend to bundled OpenSSL and using HTTP/1.1 with TLS 1.2 completed the push with certificate validation intact. This repository now keeps those transport settings locally; the fix changes Git's TLS client implementation, not the proxy app or its settings. When network requests fail, check the app-specific route, credentials, endpoint/protocol and request size separately; retry a narrow read with a bounded retry, and do not infer the configured proxy itself is at fault from one failed request.

2026-11-02 PartySize map-dialogue recovery: if a general Native MapState conversation appears during settlement travel, PartySize now offers only the currently clickable original `dialogueSelect` choices, or `dialogueContinue` when the Native options array is empty. The Host validates the exact current option ID and still keeps the StoryMode VillagersInNeed Mission exception separate. This lets the Agent handle current map text before resuming recruitment; it does not automate arbitrary Mission dialogs, accept unrelated quests, or alter Campaign time. `TestResults/20261102-025502` covers the two catalog/validation cases, and `TestResults/20261102-030407` extends the Fake Native loop through an active move, a map conversation option, resumed movement, and the known StoryMode Village Mission/recruitment route. Both AgentHostPackage runs pass with zero real-model calls and zero Native gameplay commands; natural EYC/model travel remains unverified.

2026-11-02 PartySize move-wait efficiency: during a safe active EYC settlement move, the provider request now constrains `observeAgain.waitSeconds` to exactly 10 and the Host rejects any other value. The Native movement monitor remains responsible for once-per-second passive checks and can return early on arrival/interruption; this control does not change Campaign time. Packaged Host regression `TestResults/20261102-020621` passes with zero real-model calls and zero gameplay commands. `TestResults/20261102-021713` adds a negative Host case: the fake provider first returns 9 seconds, verifies rejection without Native polling, then retries with 10 and completes after Native monitor polling. `TestResults/20261102-022142` verifies the generic structured-output schema and rejects 9/11 locally. `TestResults/20261102-022826` carries the exact bounds through a stubbed OpenAI-compatible `required_tool_call` HTTP payload and validates the 10-second returned decision; no request leaves the process. The policy is not yet measured in a permitted live-model run.

2026-11-02 Companion map-dialogue interruption: the Tavern-hiring subloop now suppresses its map movement wait when a MapState conversation is active and offers only current clickable Native replies, or Continue when no choice is clickable. The action resolver validates map options separately from the Tavern hiring option allowlist. `TestResults/20261102-031833` revealed that the new Continue check accidentally captured the adjacent no-argument `missionSceneAgents` switch label; the branch was split to preserve the existing Tavern startup path. `TestResults/20261102-032254` AgentHostPackage passed with two new Companion map-dialogue contract cases, zero real-model calls and zero Native gameplay commands. Natural Companion travel/model behavior remains unverified.

2026-11-02 CampaignLoop MapState dialogue interruption: the generic map-arrival loop now exposes only current clickable Native replies or no-choice Continue while a non-encounter map conversation is active, including during movement monitoring and safe arrival refresh. It dispatches through the audited BattleSimulation dialogue transport and rechecks current state after each response; if no compatible Native transport exists, it does not substitute another action. `TestResults/20261102-033741` AgentHostPackage passed with 24 CampaignLoop self-tests/312 Provider scenarios, zero model calls and zero Native commands. No natural EYC safe-settlement-refresh conversation was exercised.

2026-11-01 direct Gang Leader recruit hand-in: the installed Native path uses PartyScreenMode.TroopsManage, distinct from QuestTroopManage used for companion alternatives. Bind the screen to the active GangLeaderNeedsRecruitsIssueQuest and current conversation Hero; use its Native VM handlers and keep staged transfer count separate from committed Journal progress. Settlement moves also require the Native marker-face path gate before invoking SettlementVisual.OnMapClick; an accepted command is not proof of travel. Bounded Town rests must start from the Native Town menu and set their time target atomically, then pause on completion or interruption before returning control.

2026-11-01 Native trade-confirm mailbox: when the original Trade Done handler opens an Inquiry, the CLI now supports returning its `accepted` receipt immediately via `-ReturnWhenAccepted`, so an operator can inspect/respond to the exact Native prompt rather than waiting for a transaction that has not yet been confirmed. The later GoldSale Host integration is described in the following entry; this CLI correction by itself did not complete autonomous trade confirmation.

2026-11-01 Gold-sale Native capacity Inquiry: the trade adapter now binds a pending Done confirmation to the exact Native Yes/No inquiry token. The GoldSale Host accepts only an `accepted` `tradeConfirm` followed by a current Trade Inventory inquiry, resolves the visible choice through the shared Native inquiry decision, verifies the original Gold/item/merchant/time changes after Yes, and after No verifies no transaction then calls Native `tradeCancelChanges` to close the still-open draft. It does not replay the sale. Isolated Mod and Host builds passed with the Bannerlord client left open, and the Host provider contract self-test passed with 0 real model calls/0 Native commands. The existing manual `TestResults/20261101-063917/Temp/NativeInquiryTradeEvidence.json` covers the original UI Yes, No, and cancel branches; the new Host/adapter integration is not yet verified in a newly loaded game or by an LLM.

2026-11-01 Battle Loot Done Inquiry transport: BattleSimulation's `partyScreenDone` can return `accepted` while the original PartyState asks whether to leave the remaining left-side roster behind. NativeMailboxClient and Agent-Cli now return that receipt immediately; the existing BattleSimulation loop then rereads the current state and uses its shared inquiry resolver for the model's goal-aware Yes/No choice. Its fake Native loop self-test now exercises `accepted`→current PartyState Inquiry→model choice→resume and confirms the Done action plus response are both counted; both accepted transport paths and the Host loop passed in `TestResults/20261101-154532/Temp/AgentHostProviderSelfTest-latest.json` (302 scenarios, 0 real model calls/0 Native game commands). The manual Campaign's 21/21-capacity case rejected a second peasant transfer and Native Done closed directly to Inventory without a Yes/No Inquiry. The spare-capacity accepted-Inquiry branch still needs a newly loaded DLL and runtime/model verification.

2026-11-01 BattleSimulation accepted Done receipt: `NativeMailboxClient.ExecuteBattleSimulationActionAsync` now waits for `accepted` only on `partyScreenDone`; the Host counts both an accepted Done action and its later Native Inquiry response, then resumes from the fresh Party/Inventory state. A deterministic Fake Native loop now returns an accepted Done receipt before the current PartyState Inquiry and verifies the full decision/resume path and action count. The isolated Host provider self-test passed with 0 model calls/0 Native game commands. On the manual campaign, the full-capacity branch behaved differently: one peasant transfer filled the 21/21 MainParty; Done closed to Loot Inventory without a Yes/No Inquiry, leaving the final peasant behind. Thus this runtime confirms the full-capacity Native branch, while the spare-capacity accepted Inquiry branch remains package-tested only; the current game process still has the prior DLL loaded.

2026-11-01 Host travel interruption contract: during an existing EYC settlement move, the Host polls the existing Native `mapPartyTargets` query at one-second intervals inside its bounded wait, without another model decision. A currently visible hostile Bandit causes a separately recorded Native Pause; the child returns the fresh paused map state, and the EYC parent routes it through the existing BattleSimulation loop only if that current state still verifies the hostile party and original settlement move. Neutral parties do not trigger a pause. This adds no Bridge action and does not hide clock control inside `move`. `TestResults/20261101-020340` and `022207` Preflight/Build/AgentHostPackage passed; fake cases cover hostile pause, neutral no-pause, and the parent's exact interruption-code gate while preserving the normal active-encounter route. Tests made zero model calls. Natural campaign runtime interruption remains unverified.

2026-11-01 PartySize request cost reduction: the Recruitment observation previously repeated the same prior Native receipt both inside `observation.previousNativeReceipt` and at the canonical top-level `previousActionReceipt`. The observation now omits the duplicate and keeps the top-level receipt. The AgentHostPackage self-test synthesizes a 4 KB receipt and verifies the request saves at least that duplicate payload while retaining the canonical receipt; package tests passed without model calls. This reduces repeated prompt tokens, but actual DeepSeek cache/cost savings must be measured in a future permitted real-model run.

2026-11-01 PartySize prompt-prefix ordering: the stable journal/objective/visible-safe-settlement fields now precede the changing Native map state in the serialized observation; the changing `iteration` field is last in the request. The packaged DeepSeek tokenizer contract compares two movement observations with the same 32 visible targets but different map position values. It measured an 854-token increase in the exact shared input prefix (647→1501) for this synthetic pair; the AgentHostPackage receipt now preserves this diagnostic metric. This is an upper-bound cache opportunity, not a provider cache-hit or billed-cost result. DeepSeek cache still must be measured during a permitted real-model run.

2026-11-01 Gold-sale prompt-prefix ordering: the stable StoryMode Gold objective and prior receipt now precede the changing Native Map state; the changing iteration is last. Its package self-test measured an exact shared-prefix increase from 677 to 770 tokens (+93) across a synthetic moving-map pair. `TestResults/20261101-024918/AgentHostPackage.log` retains both PartySize (+854) and Gold-sale (+93) tokenizer estimates in machine-readable result fields. This does not prove actual DeepSeek cache hits or billing savings.

2026-11-01 Live movement-monitor probe: the dedicated `AgentHostNativeMapMovementMonitorProbe` Harness phase requires an already-running disposable `ChronicleW7P-manual-*` campaign with an active EYC settlement move and no Host process. It builds only the .NET AgentHost project, then invokes the production movement-monitor class over the existing Native mailbox. It never calls a model; a separate Native Pause is recorded if a hostile Bandit is currently visible. `TestResults/20261101-041215/Temp/NativeMapMovementMonitorProbe.json` confirms 8 actual Native `mapPartyTargets` polls during an 8-second window; Campaign time advanced normally from day 91129.877 to 91129.991, with `StoppablePlay` before/after. No Bandit was visible, so this run verifies polling through the live mailbox but does not verify the hostile-detection/Pause branch. `TestResults/20261101-041442/Temp/AgentHostPackage/AgentHostPackageManifest.json` then passed the Host self-test with 299 provider scenarios, 23 Campaign-loop scenarios and 21 BattleSimulation-loop scenarios; it made zero model calls and zero Native gameplay commands. The full Harness package phase correctly refused its game-module Build stage while Bannerlord remained open; the Host-only package test passed without closing the disposable campaign. The probe does not create/load/save a campaign, choose an objective, pursue a party or claim combat success.
