A synthetic no-model test checks correlation and redaction. Contract version 7.548.

Read-only FreeLLMAPI catalog inspection found 734 model rows, including `llm7-fast-router` as `needsKey`/disabled, not an immediately usable route. LLM7's official Terms describe a no-charge token allowance up to 100,000 tokens per rolling day, but function calling and JSON mode are Pro features; the Host can only try its existing prompt-only JSON contract on a free model after a token is configured and the model is listed ready. The same Terms position the service for education/research and prohibit unapproved downstream redistribution, so this is only a possible personal test route—not a product default. References: https://github.com/chigwell/llm7.io/blob/main/TERMS.md and https://github.com/tashfeenahmed/freellmapi/blob/main/docs/en/providers/01-supported-platforms.md.

The first no-auth GET to LLM7's catalog through the configured SOCKS proxy timed out after 10 seconds. Two later read-only GETs to `https://api.llm7.io/v1/models` succeeded over that proxy (HTTP 200 in 0.82–0.83 seconds, both remote and local DNS modes), so the earlier timeout was transient and the host/route is reachable. The public catalog lists `gpt-oss:20b` as a `turbo` model with JSON capability and 98.32% recent availability, but LLM7's free token tier does not include Pro's forced tool/JSON mode. FreeLLMAPI still marks its LLM7 router `needsKey`/disabled; a user-owned free token must be added locally before one bounded prompt-only JSON test. Do not send the token in chat or configure paid routes. Its free-token quota and terms are documented at https://github.com/chigwell/llm7.io/blob/main/TERMS.md.

The no-auth AI Horde status endpoint, queried through the same proxy without inference, returned 22 text-model rows and zero workers available. Together with the earlier `freeLlmApiPoolExhausted` 429, AI Horde currently cannot serve the selected Gemma route; require a new worker-status observation before trying it again.

Live model phases now run a read-only configured-provider readiness preflight before Build and Smoke. It supports FreeLLMAPI or an explicitly configured remote OpenAI-compatible HTTPS endpoint; if the selected route/model is missing or unavailable, the Harness stops before launching Bannerlord or sending an inference request. This also permits the user's explicit DeepSeek canary without changing the provider-neutral product architecture. Contract version 7.552.

Negative preflight evidence: `TestResults/20261106-085057` explicitly selected `llm7-fast-router` against the configured loopback endpoint. Native/Harness environment Preflight passed; FreeLLMAPIModelPreflight rejected the route because it was absent from `available=true`. The stage list contains no Build, Smoke, or PersistentEycCampaign; no Bannerlord/Host process was started and no inference request was sent. A preceding `20261106-084957` probe omitted the process-local base URL and correctly stopped at endpoint validation; it is not evidence about model availability.

2026-11-06 DeepSeek bounded follow-up: Provider smoke `TestResults/20261106-174917` passed with one 780/64-token `deepseek-flash` goal-classification request (640 cache-hit prompt tokens; estimated ¥0.00098112; zero Native actions); AgentHostProvider's 347 deterministic scenarios also passed. The recruitment target-cache contract now preserves `null` as “not queried” and only filters a known Native target array; its regression requires `mapSettlementTargets` to reappear after an invalidated cache. Persistent run `20261106-175333` resumed the hash-verified EYC test save, spent 5 model decisions / 13,380 prompt / 275 completion tokens / estimated ¥0.024817152, and checkpointed; it selected PartySize and reached `village_V2_2`, then the per-continuation decision cap expired after the read-only `recruitOptions` query, before the no-offer leave action. Continuation `20261106-175913` spent 4 decisions / 10,834 prompt / 230 completion tokens / estimated ¥0.016769472, then the ¥0.05 per-run reservation guard correctly refused the next request. The model selected Renown, left the village and queried `mapPartyTargets`; therefore this runtime sample did not exercise the PartySize cache-refresh branch. Both checkpoints were saved to Chronicle-owned disposable saves; the original EYC quest remains incomplete and game processes were cleaned. The null/refresh regression passed in the no-model AgentHost contract run, but its actual Campaign branch remains unverified. Total estimated DeepSeek cost for these three bounded runs was about ¥0.04257; do not spend another inference request on this branch until a cheaper targeted runtime setup is available.

2026-11-06 follow-through on Native recruitment and resumed movement: `TestResults/20261106-183008` continued the same natural EYC campaign for 40 DeepSeek decisions (122,915 prompt / 2,381 completion tokens; estimated ¥0.210075552; reservation ceiling ¥0.50), 18 Native actions. The no-offer village path now visibly worked: after `recruitOptions` returned no usable offer, the model chose the original Leave option and the Host exposed/re-executed `mapSettlementTargets`; it then selected new currently visible safe settlements. This verifies the cache fix in Campaign. Journal stayed Gold 0/2000, PartySize 1/25, Renown 20/50, Companions 1/1; the PartySize child reached its 40-decision cap during live movement. The manifest checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-20261106183242-b3e8e110` is a Chronicle-owned test save.

The next 160-decision continuation `TestResults/20261106-183929` made zero model calls/actions because the checkpoint contained an in-flight Native settlement move with `timeControlMode=Stop`. `AgentHostCampaignLoop` rejected this before even performing its read-only goal classification, despite the EYC autonomy parent already having a bounded, source-verified same-target Native Move recovery. The guard now blocks only after goal classification: an EYC request can enter that recovery, while already-classified ordinary map movement stays blocked and no explicit clock change is introduced. `TestResults/20261106-184750` AgentHostPackage passed the regression (351 provider-contract, 26 Campaign-loop and 26 BattleSimulation scenarios; zero model/Native gameplay calls). Live continuation `TestResults/20261106-185150` now provides runtime evidence: the stopped checkpoint was classified as EYC, the parent reissued the same currently visible Native Move, the subsequent Native observation showed `StoppablePlay` and CampaignDay advanced from 91119.603 to 91119.617, then model decisions/actions resumed. This proves the saved-move recovery handoff works in Campaign without explicit clock control.

That continuation used 71 accepted DeepSeek decisions / 130 Native actions, 222,367 prompt and 4,523 completion tokens, estimated ¥0.405198624 and reserved ¥0.8594928. It exercised repeated safe-settlement recruitment searches and hostile interruptions; no EYC Journal target changed (Gold 0/2000, PartySize 1/25, Renown 20/50, Companions 1/1). Four invalid `provider-control-unavailable` responses were rejected before Native actions; the Host recovered with the current catalog. The run ended on HTTP 503 before another action: `Server=openresty`, JSON error type/code `service_unavailable_error`, no Retry-After, request ID, or response body logged; the response passed through the selected local listener `127.0.0.1:7890`, which does not identify which hop produced it. Harness preflight, build, package, capture, checkpoint save, fixture integrity, and process cleanup all passed. The original EYC remains incomplete; do not call this a product or objective pass.

Read-only follow-up loaded the same hash-verified disposable save `ChronicleEycDev-20261106185416-5d76cff7` (`590AA834B2E9C11E59556C4DACD9C4EAB2A491F07203EC50E8C147545D4E9B7C`) and stopped without issuing a Campaign action; the save hash stayed unchanged. Native state was `town_V2`, active EYC, Gold 0/2000, PartySize 1/25 (0 healthy, 1 wounded), Renown 20/50, Companions 1/1, food -0.05, foodInventory 0, `isStarving=true`, Morale 0. The Town menu showed enabled `town_wait` and `town_streets`; `settlementOverlayCharacters` showed GangLeader Daliset with current `AvailableIssue` “帮派需要招新”. The authoritative J research and adapter already verify its direct Native handoff requires ordinary Bandit-occupation troops, not prisoners, and pays by tier; however the EYC PartySize executor currently searches volunteer/prisoner recruitment and does not surface this Issue route. At observation time, bounded Native `rest` was scoped only to the separate Supply loop; the EYC rest-recovery implementation and its live-verification status are recorded immediately below. The gang-leader Issue remains a candidate EYC PartySize/Gold route, not an executed or verified continuation.

The follow-up disposable rest probe used the same Native Town menu and a requested bounded `rest(Days=3)`. The native action was accepted, but the blocking `Agent-Cli rest` call timed out after its 45-second command deadline before the three daily ticks were observed; the owned game process was stopped, engine config restored and the source save hash remained unchanged. Therefore no wound/healing result is known, and this is not evidence that Native rest itself failed. A first probe with an incorrect harness precondition was stopped before sending rest because the in-settlement `encounterActive` flag is true for a normal Town menu; the second probe used the Native menu and `town_wait` condition. Future validation must use the existing Host-managed bounded-rest monitor (which polls until the target daily tick and then selects current Native `wait_leave`), not the blocking CLI call.

Corrected Native probe: using `rest(Days=0.6, ReturnWhenAccepted=true)` plus read-only polling to the target tick and the current Native `wait_leave`, the same disposable EYC save advanced CampaignDay 91123.581→91124.182 (tick 46→47), changed MainParty from healthy0/wounded1 to healthy1/wounded0, and returned to `town`/`Stop`. Food remained -0.05, foodInventory 0, Morale 0; the source save hash stayed `590AA834...`, and the Host stopped with engine config restored. This demonstrates a short wait can restore the sole wounded MainHero even while starving, but no general healing rate is inferred. The EYC parent now has an emergency bounded Native-rest recovery for a completely wounded party when a safe Town menu exposes `town_wait`; it runs once per unchanged health/party/settlement fingerprint, re-reads state, and never issues an explicit clock action. Its gate deliberately permits the observed Native `isWaiting=true` while `menuId=town` and `timeControlMode=Stop`; `isWaitMenu` is the actual open Wait screen. New source/self-test changes still require Build/AgentHostPackage/Docs and live EYC verification before relying on this path.

DeepSeek continuation evidence (`TestResults/20261106-214427`, `214910`, `215704`; same Campaign ID `05896845153e4f019caeb20ebb3603c1`): the first 1-decision cap spent ¥0.001612896 to classify the goal as EYC and had no decision left for a Native action; this exposed that a one-decision continuation is not a meaningful gameplay test. The 3-decision retry spent ¥0.009410688, selected Renown and queried `mapPartyTargets`, then could not proceed because no safe target/action was present. The 8-decision continuation spent ¥0.041774496 / reserved ¥0.0832704 and executed 7 Native actions. It repeated the Renown scan with no safe Bandit, selected PartySize, moved to `town_V2`, ran the verified 0.6-day Native rest recovery (0 healthy/1 wounded→1 healthy/0 wounded), queried volunteer offers, and used the current Native Town Leave after no offer. At `town_V2`, `settlementOverlayCharacters` visibly reported Gang Leader Daliset with AvailableIssue “帮派需要招新”, but the EYC PartySize action catalog did not expose contacting that giver, so the model could not evaluate this already source/runtime-contracted Native recruitment Issue. No EYC Journal target changed (Gold0/2000, PartySize1/25, Renown20/50, Companions1/1); the continuation ended at its decision cap and is not a gameplay pass. The hash-verified Chronicle-owned checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-20261106215932-d1adb4c4` (`4CDA047AA8363B4F04EC928FB7045F551406ED5D5343A56BBCB2300FD992C264`) remains selected; visual capture covered 137/137 frames and reduced the recording to indexed keyframes, and game/Host processes were cleaned. This evidence supports, but does not prove, the existing implementation gap: compose the already verified GangLeader direct Issue and `TroopsManage` handoff with the EYC PartySize/Gold routes. The Issue is optional and its acceptance or completion is not an EYC requirement; do not count its marker as accepted or as progress.

EYC rest recovery implementation: `AgentHostEstablishYourClanAutonomyLoop` now intercepts only the exact no-healthy-member/wounded-party state at a stopped Native Town menu with the current enabled `town_wait` option. It calls the existing bounded-rest Native adapter for 0.6 days, waits for the requested CampaignDay (not merely the next daily tick), selects the current `wait_leave` handler and returns fresh health/food values; one unchanged failed recovery fingerprint cannot loop. This is an internal emergency recovery before objective planning, not a new Journal objective and not an explicit Campaign clock API. `TestResults/20261106-211312` AgentHostPackage passes (353 provider-contract, 26 CampaignLoop, 26 BattleSimulation scenarios; 0 model/Native calls). The first live EYC verification is recorded below.

Live follow-up `TestResults/20261106-201301` continued from the same hash-verified save but did not show the expected 0.6-day rest before the first planner/Map action (Native observation remained near CampaignDay 91123.819 versus the checkpoint's 91123.581). A one-time redacted `nativeRestRecoveryEligibility` progress event was added before the check to expose the exact gate state on the next continuation; it proved that run loaded a free MapState, not the Town menu, so the parent correctly deferred recovery.

The focused continuation `TestResults/20261106-212102` then reached a Native Town from the PartySize child and passed the emergency recovery through the same Host-managed bounded Native rest: objective subflow `nativeRestRecovery` completed, CampaignDay 91123.821→91124.421, DailyTick 47, Party health 0→1 healthy and 1→0 wounded; food remained negative and morale 0. This verifies the rest handoff in real EYC, with no model calls during the wait and no explicit clock action. DeepSeek subsequently had 27 decisions / 83,131 prompt / 1,649 completion tokens / estimated ¥0.144897696 under the ¥0.30 reservation cap. No EYC Journal value changed; the Renown route saw no safe Bandit and PartySize was interrupted by hostile parties. The outer Harness could not verify/save a new checkpoint, so the prior hash-verified save remains authoritative. Next product gap is to expose the currently visible GangLeader “帮派需要招新” Issue as an optional Native PartySize/Gold opportunity using the existing issue and quest troop transfer contracts; do not claim it until the full accept→recruit ordinary Bandits→handoff→Journal/reward result is implemented and tested.

2026-11-06 implementation of that Host gap: while EYC PartySize is active, the model can now contact only a current settlement-overlay Notable whose visible AvailableIssue/ActiveIssue title identifies “帮派需要招新” / “Gang Leader Needs Recruits”. Once one AvailableIssue giver has been contacted and declined, the Host suppresses repeat contact while that marker remains AvailableIssue; an ActiveIssue marker remains eligible so the same giver can be revisited for delivery. During that contacted conversation, the current clickable direct Issue route and neutral exits remain available, while the known companion and Lord alternatives are removed and also rejected by the resolver because it now requires the selected OptionId to be in the exact current Host catalog. If Native opens the original `PartyState/TroopsManage` with `issueTroopAssignment.kind=gangLeaderRecruitHandoff`, the EYC parent reuses the same Native `PartyCharacterVM` transfer and `PartyVM` Done/Cancel handlers already used for `QuestTroopManage`; the screen contract exposes only Native-transferable current MainParty member rows, honors the live assignment limit, permits partial Done only when Native enables it, and returns to the parent for a fresh EYC Journal check. Screen handling uses the shared run decision budget rather than its former fixed eight-decision cap, so the target is not inferred from the manual seven-recruit example. No vanilla mechanic or Native action was added. `TestResults/20261106-224922` Build and AgentHostPackage pass; package Provider self-tests exercise visible-marker filtering, one-time AvailableIssue contact, ActiveIssue delivery contact, direct-option-only selection, rejection of alternatives/non-current options, and a 12-member dynamic handoff limit. These are deterministic Host contracts; the new real-model/native route has not yet been exercised in Campaign, and the EYC Journal remains unchanged.

DeepSeek follow-up `TestResults/20261106-225509` exercised the updated live route for 12 decisions / 40,252 prompt / 720 completion tokens, estimated ¥0.071303808 under the ¥0.15 run limit. At `town_V2`, the Native observation carried Daliset's current AvailableIssue marker and the real model action catalog contained both `talkToSettlementHero` for that exact Hero and the Native Leave option. With Gold0, PartySize1/25 and no prisoner roster, the model first stopped; after the Host's bounded feedback that safe current actions remained, it chose the original `town_leave` rather than accepting the Issue. This is evidence that the route is actually exposed and the model can decline it; it is not evidence of direct issue acceptance, troop delivery, payout or EYC progress. The model then selected another safe settlement, issued original Native movement and a 10-second passive observation. The run ended at its configured decision cap with Gold0/2000, PartySize1/25, Renown20/50, Companions1/1; checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-20261106225737-6bf30b3c` (`D800B9109C0E66FC23C39FDB1475EB76F1C54A66EE89BCEEF19F72801969B1D1`) was saved, and game/Host cleanup passed. Whether this model refusal is appropriate with an empty Bandit roster remains a strategy question for subsequent evidence; do not force acceptance to claim feature coverage.

Fresh natural campaign run `TestResults/20261106-231920` verified original Tutorial skip, natural EYC activation and model-driven clan name/banner/inquiry setup, then completed the EYC Companion Journal target through original Tavern hiring (1/1). It made 42 model decisions / 95 Native actions and estimated ¥0.24916671; PartySize reached 2 total members and current gold was 410 after the hiring/travel segment, while Renown remained20. It stopped at the provider reservation guard during PartySize travel; Gold/PartySize/Renown EYC targets remained unmet and `StoryModeOriginalEstablishYourClanCompleted=false`. The phase is `Passed` only for its declared fresh-start/bootstrap slice, not a full EYC pass. It was nonpersistent (`NoSaveRequested=true`), so cannot serve as a continuation checkpoint.

Second-campaign branch `freshB` Harness history: the first seed attempt `TestResults/20261106-233711` failed because its SaveAs-ready signal was emitted only after the real-model Host ended inside a Tavern Mission. The seed saver only accepts Native MapState. The seed path now starts in Native-only mode, emits SaveAs-ready as soon as original Tutorial Skip, natural EYC activation and stable MapState are confirmed, and then starts the real-model continuation only after Native SaveAs verifies Campaign ID, time/mode and hash. `PersistentEycCampaignKey` isolates manifests and `ChronicleEycDev-<key>-*` saves, preserving the primary campaign and other branches.

The next run `TestResults/20261107-010418` verified that early seed SaveAs, then resumed the fresh campaign. DeepSeek made 22 decisions / 42 Native actions (¥0.11729485 estimated, ¥0.249036 reserved) and stopped at its cost reservation limit in `MapState/town_backstreet`. The continuation checkpoint failed because the Harness only accepted `town`/`village`/`castle` menus; the original Backstreet menu had the current enabled Native `town_backstreet_back` Leave, matched the current non-sieged town, and was outside the Tavern Mission. The seed checkpoint is still intact and selected. The Host checkpoint validator now accepts this exact Backstreet menu only when its current native Back option is enabled, the current settlement is a non-sieged town, and no Mission/modal/battle screen is active; deterministic checks reject a disabled Back option, siege, or MissionState. `AgentHostPackage` and `Docs` still need to pass after this validator change, then the same `freshB` branch should resume from its verified seed and confirm a new Native checkpoint.

The `TestResults/20261106-231920` natural model evaluation also completed the Companion journal target (1/1) while leaving Gold, PartySize and Renown unmet. It is an independent StoryMode start/bootstrap slice, not a full EYC pass. The primary older natural campaign and the freshB branch both remain incomplete; the two-campaign EYC gate is still open.

2026-11-07 `freshB` resumed-continuation check (`TestResults/20261107-013048`) verified the checkpoint correction: from `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107012611-06724d29` (SHA-256 `09B245B7211DEC92D46D0A091A9CBFF952AE492E235920628AE7864DBE35C4FE`), DeepSeek made 21 decisions / 29 Native actions (¥0.118302624 estimated, ¥0.242124 reserved) and reached the previously rejected `MapState/town_backstreet` after a Companion route interruption/budget failure. The Harness classified the Native enabled Back option + matching safe non-sieged town as `native-settlement-menu`, completed Native SaveAs, and retained the new checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107013313-6da26634` (SHA-256 `10C007BE9445E44695095C31AE8AC0331EE85784E4BB6D6F2B5B63ED0C768820`) with the same Campaign ID. EYC still had no verified target completion; the freshB branch now has a resumable, hash-verified post-action checkpoint. Game and Host processes were clean. 2026-11-07 `freshB` cost-bounded DeepSeek continuation (`TestResults/20261107-025504`) resumed the same natural Campaign instance. The prior continuation `TestResults/20261107-024023` had spent 88 model decisions / 124 Native actions / ¥0.46262125 estimated (¥0.9996408 reserved under a ¥1 ceiling) without completing an EYC target; it stopped before another request would exceed that ceiling and saved a verified checkpoint. To constrain the follow-up, the process explicitly set a ¥0.10 run ceiling, 128 output tokens and one HTTP request per decision. DeepSeek made 9 decisions / 7 Native actions (29,832 prompt / 515 completion tokens; ¥0.05275741 estimated, ¥0.093384 reserved) before the reservation guard correctly refused the next request. The model stayed in the Companion-hiring scope, visited the same town/tavern route, and ended with Gold 1000, PartySize 1, Renown 20 and no hired Companion; this did not advance the EYC Journal. Native map screenshot and full 101/101-frame clip coverage were captured; the temporary video was removed after keyframe extraction. Native SaveAs produced `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107025734-b28887b1` (SHA-256 `3AD4653931AE056DD5047F642F9E5D0C4DD7FA5F714770DADA2871391BA9CA42`) in the same Campaign instance; save hash verification passed and game/Host processes were clean. The low-cost run confirms the provider and budget guard work, but also confirms the current Companion subflow can spend calls repeating an unproductive settlement loop. Do not repeat that loop with another model call until its target/action selection or stopping behavior changes. EYC remains incomplete; this is neither a gameplay pass nor a full-goal validation.

After that live run, the Companion executor was changed so an exact Native Tavern Mission scene roster is fetched as read-only observation before the next model decision (only when no conversation is active and the Native companion target is not already met). The real model still selects navigation, interaction, and visible conversation responses. The package self-test verifies two such Native scene reads add zero provider decisions and that the model still completes the simulated Native hire path; Build and AgentHostPackage pass at `TestResults/20261107-032130`. This is a cost-path regression result only, not evidence of real model token savings or of EYC gameplay progress. A later bounded live run must measure the actual savings before any cost claim; resume from the current hash-verified `freshB` checkpoint only after the broader objective-selection loop is improved, rather than replaying the same no-candidate tavern route.

Campaign-continuity increment: the C# Host now reads/writes the existing Native Campaign save checkpoint and stores a bounded list of Tavern towns where a current Native scene scan returned zero eligible Wanderers. The memory is bound to the saved Campaign identity and exact four-objective Journal snapshot; any Journal progress change invalidates it. It is recorded only after the Tavern mission returns to MapState, so a partial UI action is not represented as durable progress. If the exact current Tavern catalog contains no eligible Wanderer and only the original Native `missionLeave`, the Host submits that one unambiguous exit without a model call, then stores the negative observation at MapState; navigation, interaction, hire dialogue and any multi-option decision remain model-controlled. `TestResults/20261107-040422` AgentHostPackage's Fake Native test spans two Host instances, records an empty `town_a` roster and verifies only `town_b` remains selectable on resume. The Native save/API was also used in real Campaign continuations: `TestResults/20261107-041139` scanned Town ES3 and continued toward Town EW2; its checkpoint was `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107041407-7b7149c4` (SHA-256 `D3581AFB22DA8CEAFA9753666D963623172C84530998D8F444C087DAA0A95C92`). On next load, `TestResults/20261107-043410` reported `checkpointReadAttempted=true`, `noCandidateTownCount=1`, `memoryMatchedCurrentJournal=true`, and `memoryInvalidatedByJournalProgress=false`; thus the C# checkpoint memory survived a Native SaveAs/load boundary in the same Campaign. That run spent 10 decisions / 14 Native commands, estimated ¥0.03797388 (¥0.0841176 reserved), but the model kept requesting `mapSettlementTargets` and repeating `move` to the already active Town EW2 order after hostile-party interruptions. EYC targets remained unchanged. The memory path is live-verified; objective/gameplay completion is not.

To remove that repeated move, the Companion child now inherits an already active Native `GoToSettlement` target after a parent-level interruption and waits on that original movement/encounter state instead of asking the model for another target query and duplicate move. Package self-test at `TestResults/20261107-044809` passes a case where an existing move reaches Town EW2; it verifies the model receives the Town menu after arrival and the Host sends zero extra Native movement actions. Live DeepSeek continuation `TestResults/20261107-050137` then resumed from the saved move target: after two hostile-Bandit interrupts, the Companion subflow read back the same Native EW2 target and did not issue duplicate EW2 `mapSettlementTargets`/`move` decisions. It arrived at EW2, found no eligible Wanderer, stored the second empty-town observation, and selected EW3. It made 10 decisions / 22 Native commands (¥0.04515229 estimated / ¥0.091908 reserved); no Journal target changed. Native SaveAs retained `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107050404-225d32bb` (SHA-256 `EC71047B222F8A18C5D47DB43216CCF03078D03FD9D434A4B9240C13CFC7FED9`) in the same Campaign with game/Host cleanup. This live run verifies restart-safe negative-Tavern memory and interrupted-move continuation, not companion recruitment or EYC progress. The first full natural-Campaign gate remains open.

Cause verified from `TestResults/20261107-043410`: the saved party already had Native `targetSettlementId=town_EW2`, `defaultBehavior=GoToSettlement`, and `isMoving=true` in `StoppablePlay`. Two successive visible-Bandit interrupts restarted the Companion child; its local `moveTargetId` reset, so the model repeatedly requested `mapSettlementTargets` and selected the same EW2 Move, even while CampaignDay advanced. The `freshB` checkpoint memory loaded and matched the current Journal (`noCandidateTownCount=1`); the same empty tavern was not visited again. The movement continuation fix is now confirmed by `TestResults/20261107-050137`. A separate Harness false failure at `20261107-045324` was only the 90-second `--provider-self-test` timeout; after raising its wait limit to 180 seconds, the unchanged assertions passed at `20261107-045738`. Harness-only timeout adjustment is documented in DEVELOPMENT, not a product issue.

The same `TestResults/20261107-050137` trace exposed a further policy-ordering gap: the movement monitor had paused for a visible hostile Bandit, but the EYC parent handled the result first as a generic paused move and replanned Companion; its later existing `BattleSimulation` branch never ran. The parent now gives a fresh, Native-confirmed hostile-Bandit pause priority over generic paused-move recovery, while ordinary pauses with no visible hostile still return to the existing original-Move recovery. `TestResults/20261107-051813` Build/AgentHostPackage passes a contract test for this precedence. The next bounded real Campaign continuation must verify the actual sequence reaches BattleSimulation and avoids another Companion-planning loop; current EYC Journal remains unchanged.

Real follow-up `TestResults/20261107-053329` verified that route using the same freshB Campaign and a ¥0.25 ceiling. The Native BattleSimulation path found no usable Send Troops result with a one-person MainParty, so DeepSeek selected the visible original surrender option and then accepted the original ransom inquiry; Native returned to Map, Gold 1000→670, Party stayed 1, Renown 20. This is captivity recovery, not a battle win or Renown progress. A second visible Bandit later had no safe simulation; the parent replanned to PartySize and queried the current Town volunteer route. At the ¥0.25 cap (25 model decisions / 28 Native commands; ¥0.12986693 estimated, ¥0.2436984 reserved), the run stopped before recruitment but saved the safe Town EW3 checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107053559-f8ded11f` (SHA-256 `AF306C16FE654C963188A70D00BD91E857A804B5168CC79730B0C2B88B9DF21B`) in the same Campaign; game/Host cleanup passed. Journal targets remain Gold 0/2000, PartySize 1/25, Renown 20/50, Companion 0/1. Next continuation should resume from this Town checkpoint and determine whether current volunteer recruitment makes measurable PartySize progress before any further travel.

Companion-search memory reconciliation now stores a structured EYC Journal baseline and treats downward resource movement (the observed ransom 1000→670 Gold) as a baseline refresh, not positive goal progress; upward progress or target changes still invalidate the old empty-Tavern exclusions. For existing checkpoints written by the prior format, the Host parses the bounded fingerprint to recover the old four-objective baseline before making that decision, then writes the current structured snapshot. `TestResults/20261107-064923/AgentHostPackage.log` passes the legacy-format, resource-loss, and forward-progress regression within the packaged Host self-test; this does not yet prove same-Campaign checkpoint migration or that a real continuation avoids a repeated Tavern visit. The single OpenSpec change for this cohesive product fix is `openspec/changes/eyc-companion-recovery-continuity/`; its runtime verification and PartySize progress remain pending under GitHub #130.

One same-Campaign real continuation `TestResults/20261107-065613` was bounded to 1 DeepSeek decision and ¥0.05. The model spent 1549 prompt/65 completion tokens (estimated ¥0.001632096; reserved ¥0.0066168) to classify the supported EYC goal; the Host then reached its decision cap before selecting a subgoal or issuing any Native action. The test saved the verified safe Town checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107065842-02327a26` (SHA-256 `9D7802209252BCF523648C713CE1FE33F4B15E481F12EF8FE5689C52418D6CC9`) for the same Campaign ID; EYC remains incomplete, and the capture confirms the player-visible Sargot Town menu. This is not evidence for or against Companion memory migration because the memory path was never entered. It does prove one model decision is too small a useful EYC slice: the loop first classifies the goal, then must choose a subgoal and then invoke the relevant route. The actual bounded follow-up and outcome are recorded below.

That three-decision continuation is `TestResults/20261107-070300`: DeepSeek identified EYC, independently chose Companion, and sent one Native `menuSelect town_backstreet` action. It used 7828 prompt/183 completion tokens, estimated ¥0.01301761 and reserved ¥0.0288792. The same-Campaign checkpoint read returned `noCandidateTownCount=0`, so this save lineage did not contain an old empty-Tavern record to migrate; runtime legacy migration remains untested and must not trigger a duplicate test on this fixture. The new safe checkpoint is `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107070528-323f29cf` (SHA-256 `F4BFBEC1CDEDBFACCF84B9CB94BF1522563B93AB8B9DE8DC5E588888C12686D8`); capture shows the Native backstreet/tavern menu, same Campaign ID, and clean game/Host shutdown. `TestResults/20261107-081552` was deliberately kept to 8 model decisions / ¥0.10 from the same safe Sargot source checkpoint used in `080214`. After process restart, the model again selected Companion before PartySize; the visible looter encounter interrupted it and the reservation guard stopped the next decision at `provider-budget-exhausted` (actual ¥0.037412352, 20964 prompt / 475 completion tokens). No EYC Journal growth and no new safe checkpoint resulted; source `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107080443-0a5b1e2b` remains authoritative. This is direct evidence that a fresh Host forgets the prior subgoal attempt and can choose the same unproductive route; repeated live trials should stop until the parent loop carries a Native checkpointed attempt outcome or otherwise resolves this recovery cycle. The Companion-memory OpenSpec diagnostic is complete; GitHub #130 remains the overall EYC gate.
`TestResults/20261107-081552` from the same Native Town checkpoint used only an 8-decision / ¥0.10 cap. The model again chose Companion, then a hostile Bandit interrupted movement; the budget stopped during the BattleSimulation recovery path. Actual cost ¥0.037412352 (20964 prompt / 475 completion tokens), no EYC Journal change, no safe checkpoint. The current source remains `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261107081300-dd5fe3a8` (Campaign ID unchanged); branch cumulative provider usage is 483 decisions and estimated ¥2.495247. This demonstrates that a short test continuation is too small to prove a multi-step EYC route and that a Host restart can lose the immediate route decision; it does not by itself prove an uninterrupted in-game Host repeats the same choice. I archived the completed Companion-memory continuity OpenSpec and synced its stable capability spec. Next product increment is a separate bounded, Campaign-checkpointed record of recent EYC objective attempt outcomes, exposed to the planner as evidence while leaving target selection with the model. Validate it with no-model package cases before any more DeepSeek run.

2026-11-10 freshB纵向续跑 `TestResults/20261110-191922`：Build、Smoke与AgentHostPackage通过；同一自然Campaign重载成功。真实模型累计16次决策、57,129 prompt / 951 completion tokens，费用估算¥0.101983968；预算预留器在下一请求发出前拒绝，因为预计累计¥0.2051328会超过本轮¥0.20上限。此次不是游戏崩溃。模型完成RecruitmentVM招募后选择Renown；该轮Renown子流程没有可执行的安全战斗行动目录，以`no-safe-action`结束并保存`renown=blocked / objective-route-unavailable`，随后未能再调用父级规划器选择替代目标。Native安全检查点为`ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261110192200-b115c247`（SHA-256 `721E0940A37FBE359864A0A9DAD8FCEDCE5960554865B5610BA62BBDAD4C3C5F`）。零模型调用读档核验`TestResults/20261110-192920`确认同Campaign、MapState、主队19/20、Gold64、Renown20、Clan Tier0；原版Journal为Gold64/2000、PartySize19/25、Renown20/50、Companion0/1，且持久记录renown路由不可用。下一步先检查Renown子流程的安全行动/地图遭遇候选与父规划器恢复，不立刻重跑DeepSeek；必须找出无需再次花模型钱即可验证的路由问题或降低无进展调用成本的实现。
2026-11-10 同伴负担能力记忆：同Campaign两次真实模型续跑分别在Town EW5看到同一原版报价432第纳尔；第一次Gold424，原版唯一可选项`player_companion_hire_response_2`拒付并离场，后续PartySize招募使金币降至64；第二次Host重启后仍重新与同一报价交互，16次模型决策、56,813 prompt / 909 completion tokens，估算¥0.102026592，约¥0.2076528下一次预留会超过¥0.20上限，故请求发送前停止。第二次不是游戏崩溃，Campaign安全存档成功，回读`TestResults/20261110-194900`确认同Campaign、Gold64、Party19/20、Renown20、Companion0/1、Host attempt `companions=pending`。来源：`TestResults/20261110-191922`、`20261110-194230`、`20261110-194900`。

根因已定位：Companion loop会在当前Host运行内把选了原版“暂时付不起”`player_companion_hire_response_2`的Hero加入`excludedHeroes`，但该集合未进入Campaign checkpoint；Host重启便再次允许同一个Hero进入交谈。为此新增OpenSpec `eyc-companion-affordability-memory`（父Issue #130），仅在Native拒付handler成功后持久记录HeroId和当时Gold；Gold未高于拒付时只过滤该Hero，其他酒馆同伴继续可选，Gold上升后重新开放Native报价检查。Build/AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261110-201422`通过，覆盖同Campaign checkpoint编解码、同余额/低余额、无关PartySize进度、其它候选可选和Gold上升重开，0真实模型调用/0Native游戏命令；加入checkpoint写入回执后的最终复测`TestResults/20261110-202536`再次通过。任务1.1、1.2、2.1已由`TestResults/20261110-201422`零模型AgentHostPackage覆盖；`TestResults/20261110-201948` Docs通过、归档后的最终规格校验`TestResults/20261110-202917` Docs通过，OpenSpec变更`2026-11-10-eyc-companion-affordability-memory`已归档并将两项新增要求同步至稳定`companion-search-recovery` spec。下一步可在当前同一Campaign/¥0.20上限下做一次真实模型续跑，核验拒付后记忆能否在安全MapState写入并在Host restart读取；不从新Campaign重测此局部行为。Campaign检查点仍为`ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261110194520-4e25d518`，SHA-256 `6E4998085237C6DFB54225C122ED51C4FD6BFBE935D23AA900714A198D1D53F7`。Harness contract 7.563的零模型`EycObjectiveCheckpointReadback`现在会返回未支付Wanderer记忆条数及当前Gold下实际抑制条数，但不泄露HeroId。`TestResults/20261110-204249`旧存档读回通过：同Campaign、Gold64、Party19/20、Renown20/50、Companion0/1，未支付同伴记录0，0模型费用、源存档未变。真实续跑`TestResults/20261110-204701`使用20决策/¥0.20上限，16次请求估算¥0.103037472；模型在两个Tavern没有发现可交谈Wanderer，途中被Bandit遭遇中断，原版`player_companion_hire_response_2`未出现，因此没有写入新的拒付记忆。原版安全检查点继续保存为`ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261110204932-60fe596f`（SHA-256 `775694B8E3348DE2513D2688CA861326CFB1C2A62DE24F4C80D73655EDA02663`）；零模型读回`TestResults/20261110-205843`确认同Campaign、Gold0、Party16（13健康/3伤员）、Renown20、EYC Journal Gold0/2000、Party16/25、Renown20/50、Companion0/1，目标尝试数0，未支付同伴记录0，进程清理及源档hash不变。该实测没有验证拒付记忆的原版触发，仅验证了新只读读回；下一轮从此新状态继续，先检验模型改选Gold/PartySize是否能产生Journal变化，再判断是否值得另一笔有限模型预算。单轮上限仍20 provider决策/¥0.20。

2026-11-10 active Bandit meeting no-choice correction：自然实测`TestResults/20261110-210622`确认选择Fight后Encounter仍有一条原版“点击继续”无选项文本；以前BattleSimulation loop因ActiveEncounter guard不继续，真实模型费用约¥0.098后在该UI状态后续预算耗尽，且没有安全新检查点。已在现有`ACT-G01-02/dialogueContinue`契约内移除这一过宽排除条件，只对当前MapState、当前Conversation文本非空、无clickable option调用原版Continue，并重新读取；选项出现后会先核对现行Bandit，再由模型选择。Build/AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261110-214850`通过：Fake Native/Provider从活动Encounter无选项行开始，观察Native Continue后新选项、target query、model Fight、Simulation及PostBattle；没有显式时钟动作。自然Continue之后的选项和Battle结果尚未验证。下一次实测假设：同一freshB Campaign重载后若再次看到该无选项行，AgentHostProgress应记录模型决策前一条`dialogueContinue` Native回执，随后只向模型展示新的current clickable option；Provider上限20、费用预留上限¥0.20，只有这条新代码路径实际触发时才评价本项，不重复其他战斗场景。
追击成本问题来自`TestResults/20261110-220446`：Native追击中的3次`observeAgain`占用模型预算，却没有提交新的世界动作。该证据属于GitHub #130 EYC纵向自治验收中的Host成本优化，不新建GitHub产品工作项。

追击观察器：模型提交成功的Native `pursue`后，Host在MapState、同一目标、主队移动且Native时间流动时自动只读观察，间隔1秒、最多10秒；遭遇、对话、地图事件、目标变化/丢失、队伍停下或Stop档位会提前返回。进入遭遇时Host只读刷新可见敌队身份，模型仍决定是否战斗/离开。监视不调用LLM、不发Pause/Resume/Advance/FastForward；Campaign时间由游戏原时间档自然流动。Fake Native/Provider七类边界测试与正式`AgentHostPackage`均在`TestResults/20261110-234414`通过（0真实模型调用/0 Native游戏命令），完整Docs/OpenSpec `TestResults/20261110-234924`通过。

为使真实Harness日志也能直接证明监视器何时返回，新增视觉测试专用`native-party-pursuit-passive-window`/`native-party-pursuit-encounter-target-refresh`事件；字段限于目标、停止原因、轮询数、耗时、Campaign日/时间档及固定的0模型/0时钟动作计数，不含提示词或模型回复。Fake日志回归及最终包`TestResults/20261111-000638`通过（0真实模型调用/0 Native游戏命令）。真实续跑`TestResults/20261110-235029`早于此日志补丁，行为结论来自模型决策时间戳；不为日志单独重跑付费模型。

追击监视真实样本`TestResults/20261110-235029`：相同`freshB`源存档Hash `775694B8E3348DE2513D2688CA861326CFB1C2A62DE24F4C80D73655EDA02663`自然选择追近`looters_404`，监视约10秒后Host回到模型；在Native Encounter出现前，模型付费`observeAgain`从基线3次降为1次（估算等待决策成本¥0.01557062→¥0.00504701，减少¥0.01052361）。全轮17次决策、53,079 prompt /1,045 completion tokens，实际估算¥0.09978960；与旧轮总成本¥0.09882557不是同路径可直接比较，不能声称总成本下降。随后模型重复选择Native“尝试逃离”并拒绝逃离方案，反复回到同一个Encounter菜单；下一请求预计预留后将超过¥0.20，被预算守卫拒绝，未发送新请求。Native没有形成安全检查点，旧源档仍为权威；不属游戏崩溃，游戏进程、设置恢复和捕获清理通过。失败帧可见原版Encounter菜单仍在；后续单独检查同一Try-to-get-away警告反复开启/拒绝的无进展循环，当前追击观察器只验证了模型等待调用次数减少这一局部效果。

下一项为同一Encounter的Native撤退警告防重复。`TestResults/20261110-235029`中的实际动作序列是`leave_soldiers_behind`→`try_to_get_away_reject`→回到同一Encounter并重复；没有新的遭遇或队伍变化。用双方当前Party规模和遭遇身份作临时循环内指纹；仅在指纹不变时隐藏已被拒绝的撤退选项，并把其余Native选项交还模型。状态变化后应重新开放。此为Host决策稳定性修复，不改Native时间、Encounter菜单或战斗结算，也不持久化跨档记忆；OpenSpec变更为`eyc-native-escape-warning-no-progress-guard`，仍属GitHub #130。

2026-11-11 Native撤退重试防护已实现：当Native `try_to_get_away_reject`成功并返回同一Encounter时，Host以遭遇ID、MainParty人数/健康/伤员/俘虏数量、当前可见敌队人数建立临时指纹；指纹不变时，仅从允许目录中移除 `leave_soldiers_behind`，其它可用Native选项保持由模型选择。任一方人数或遭遇对象改变后恢复该选项。`TryResolveDecision` 现同时校验菜单选项仍在当前Native目录中，防止模型提交被Host临时撤下但底层Native仍Enabled的选项。Fake Native/Provider集成路径验证了“开警告→拒绝→同一Encounter→选另一项”并继续完成BattleSimulation/战后页面；规模/对象变化重开撤退选项的契约测试通过。`TestResults/20261111-003621` AgentHostPackage通过，0真实模型调用/0Native游戏命令。下一步从同一`freshB`安全存档以最多20决策/¥0.20做一次真实续跑；只有自然遇到此警告才视为命中，随后无论结果都停止重复该配置。

更新后的真实验证`TestResults/20261111-004126`命中同一拒绝路径：第二次出现的不是逃离选项，而是恢复模式下的原版surrender。原Native `leave_soldiers_behind`仍标Enabled，Host目录已暂时抑制它并列出surrender，但旧`TryResolveDecision`仍依据Native Enabled标志拒绝surrender，导致模型重复提交该项。该轮19决策，估算成本¥0.10179456，余额/预算预留在未派发下一次请求时触顶；没有安全检查点、源档Hash未变、进程清理通过。现在validator用与目录相同的“被本轮指纹抑制”状态允许恢复模式的Native surrender回退；普通Renown模式继续拒绝。Fake验证通过原版赎金回复恢复自由Map，正式包测`TestResults/20261111-012232`通过。下一步从同一安全源存档做一次有新代码理由的真实续跑验证该路径；之后不因未遇到该分支重复配置。

2026-11-11 no-model Native Campaign exploration`TestResults/20261111-ManualMapPerception/summary.json`继续同一`freshB`源档的逐字节W7-P副本。用户确认远处地图城镇/村庄可以显示名字并直接选为目的地；`mapSettlementTargets`本轮返回395个当前Native `IsVisible`地图标记，而`nearbySettlements`只含局部一项，故Agent导航应按需用完整可见目标查询。手动路线从`town_EW3`离开后按可见入口进入Aegosca村；路上MainParty因无粮/无钱从12健康/4伤员变为11健康/5伤员。地图Incident `HuntingForaging`锁住移动，三项Native选项分别给不同肉量/经验/受伤风险；选择当前option 1后通过原版确认关掉Incident，得到2肉、没有时间增量，Party `foodInventory=2`。村庄市场没有原羊毛，只有18谷物（15金币/个）、奶酪和黄油，但Gold为0，无法补给；Sargot Artisan委托若交45羊毛可获7155，Alternative要求1名同伴、5名队员与742购买资金，均未接受。随后可见12名looters（strength 7.92）；原版`pursue`后短时间失去其可见marker、主队Hold/waiting且CampaignDay停止，之后移动时该Bandit在附近重新出现并自动触发接敌。此遇敌时MainParty仍有16人/11健康，但Native“坐镇指挥”Disabled，明确Tooltip为低士气；当前Party morale=0/effective=-6.197，源码`CanPartyAttackWithCurrentMorale`要求Morale>0。因此“有兵即可坐镇”不成立。该Encounter仅剩原版Attack/撤退/投降等处理；Native retreat预估损失7，临时测试选择后MainParty16→9，未获金币/声望，EYC仍未完成。用户已确认Battle Mission按F6可委派士兵编队但MainHero不会行动；本机零模型Controller/Formation/RTSCamera对照见本文件末尾，仍未找到能让MainHero自主移动的原版AI路径。本轮无LLM调用、无正式存档写入，源档hash不变。该运行把当前长程路线的实障碍收窄为：破产/饥饿导致低士气，进而关闭坐镇指挥；实际Campaign尚无EYC进度。

## 2026-11-11 MainHero战场AI控制验证

玩家事实：Campaign战斗中按F6只把己方部队编队交给电脑，MainHero不会因此自动行动。

本机1.4.8源码显示，单独`Agent.Controller=AI`不会自动生成新的战术目标；主角所在单人Formation已有`BehaviorGeneral`，其`CurrentOrder`为`Move`，目标随主队移动而改变。F6的原版委派作用于被选的普通部队编队，MainHero保持独立。

四次零模型原生对照均使用同一EYC源档SHA `4BCDE648…CE2E0`的可丢弃SaveAs副本，Campaign时间保持Stop，克隆按哈希删除，源存档未变且游戏退出：

- `TestResults/20261111-092652`：MainAgent Controller切AI 10秒，5次采样中位置不变、HP78；原版战斗结束后Party20→12，Renown20→22.611，Gold0。
- `TestResults/20261111-095055`：读取到`BehaviorGeneral/Move`；主角位置不变，Order目标距离约10→40米；结束于`MapState/prisoner_wait`，Party20→0，Renown20。
- `TestResults/20261111-101652`：暂时让友军Agent19接替`Mission.MainAgent`，再刷新主角的AI Formation并回调当前Move order；主角仍不动，目标距离约58→79米；恢复主角/队友Controller和原MainAgent后，Native结果仍是`prisoner_wait`、Party20→0、Renown20。
- `TestResults/20261111-105107`：按开源RTSCamera旧版源码的控制器、Alarmed WatchState、玩家单位标志和Move-order通知组合进行当前版本适配；主角仍不动，HP78→8，Native结果`prisoner_wait`、Party20→0。状态标志与Controller恢复。

本机安装的RTS Camera 5.4.16 对照：反编译 `Modules/RTSCamera/bin/Win64_Shipping_Client/RTSCamera.dll` 与 `RTSCamera.CommandSystem.dll` 后，`MissionSharedLibrary.Utilities.Utility.AIControlMainAgent`除切换`Agent.Controller`外，还处理装备相关AgentFlag、初始化`CommonAIComponent/HumanAIComponent`、解除暂停/ScriptedMovement、刷新Formation与当前MovementOrder，并可将`MissionMainAgentController`置为非激活。此处是安装包源码事实；没有把该第三方mod加入Chronicle依赖。

结论修正：Controller切换、Formation刷新和105107的RTSCamera逻辑子集均未移动主角，但它们没有复现安装版完整AI初始化；`AgentNavigator`探针也只验证其精确目标导航配置。不能把这些结果合并成“原生AI无法移动”。现按安装版RTSCamera的非相机AI handoff步骤做了Harness-only验证，结果见下段。该探针当时只验证Native租约，不是Host产品功能；后续Host整条野战闭环证据见ACT-G05-06与`TestResults/20261111-191252`。不手动注入伤亡、无敌或胜负。
Native player-input feasibility: `TestResults/20261111-113445/Temp/BattleMissionMovementInputProbe.json` used a harness-only 0.5-second retreat-relative `MovementInputVector` pulse after `MissionMainAgentController.ControlTick`, with MainAgent still Player-controlled and Campaign Stop. Position changed by 0.226 units; HP did not change during the pulse, and Campaign day/time mode stayed the same. The rest of the F6 battle later returned PartyState with Party20→17 (11 healthy/6 wounded), Renown20→22.765. This proves one short movement actuator works in that setup, not that the agent can safely navigate a battle or sustain movement without frequent model calls. Production `missionInput` remains limited to friendly Location Missions; the new probe is Harness-only and AgentHost has no such action.
Native AgentNavigator probe: `TestResults/20261111-121911/Temp/BattleMissionAgentNavigatorProbe.json` passed its Harness protocol but did not move MainHero. With the current field Battle Mission, F6-equivalent delegation, MainAgent Controller=AI, MainHero's singleton Formation AI disabled, and a reachable 8m point away from the nearest enemy, Native `SandBox.AgentNavigator` remained `GoToTarget` across three samples for 8 seconds; position and HP78 were unchanged, target distance stayed 8.01m, Campaign day and Stop mode were stable. Source SHA stayed `4BCDE648…CE2E0`; disposable clone hash matched and it was removed; game process exited; modelCalls=0. This rejects only this exact AgentNavigator configuration. The previous 0.5-second `MovementInputVector` pulse remains the only tested route that moved MainHero. No battle action was added to AgentHost. The user's F6 clarification is confirmed in the existing contract: F6 delegates troops, MainHero does not act by itself.

完整Native AI handoff首次验证：`TestResults/20261111-130714/Temp/BattleMissionNativeAiHandoffProbe.json`在同一战场克隆上重放安装版RTS Camera的AI初始化核心，不含镜头模块依赖。10秒窗口内MainHero从(578.835,597.344,22.721)移动到(585.118,638.785,18.132)，HP保持78；`MissionMainAgentController`在AI阶段非激活，租约结束后Native回读为Controller=Player、watch=Patrolling、控制器重新激活。此10秒探针随后恢复玩家控制，故其后HP变化不归于AI租约。源档hash不变，克隆按hash删除，进程停止，0模型调用。

整场原生战斗切片：`TestResults/20261111-131322/Temp/BattleMissionNativeAiHandoffProbe.json`将同一AI handoff保持到Mission结束或180秒上限。本场约57秒后进入Native Inventory/loot；AI控制期间采样16次，MainHero从(578.835,597.344,22.721)移动至(598.949,714.210,19.927)，HP78→37；随后自然结果Party20→17（11健康/6伤员）、Renown20→22.611。`MissionMainAgentController`在AI阶段非激活；Campaign日及Stop档保持不变，0模型调用。源SHA `4BCDE648…CE2E0`未变、克隆哈希验证后删除、游戏进程停止。该次单场劫匪胜利说明Native交接能让主角参与战斗，不构成跨战斗安全率；Host自动接管后来在`TestResults/20261111-191252`另行完成普通野战闭环验收。
2026-11-11 bounded DeepSeek continuation `TestResults/20261111-210328`: 8 decisions, 21,555 prompt / 452 completion tokens, estimated ¥0.037706784 (reservation ceiling ¥0.0791832 under the per-run ¥0.20 hard cap). From the prior hash-verified `freshB` checkpoint, the model selected Companion, moved toward EW6, pursued visible solo looter `looters_1133`, chose the original ultimatum/fight route, and selected Native `attack`. The one-member MainParty then entered a field Mission; Host stopped with `battle-mission-native-deployment-precondition-failed` before issuing any deployment, F6, or MainHero handoff action. Engine screenshot confirms the hero in the battle scene; failure-state Native readback says MissionState/active encounter, MainParty=1, no battle-simulation result. No verified checkpoint was saved; the prior manifest checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261111205851-4dcce16a` (SHA-256 `1190B3B37CD34435515E0920F1A8F5871F0BD417274001AF0B73DAF710FD5D76`) remains selected, the previous/source fixtures were hash-clean, and game/Host processes were cleaned. This identifies a real unsupported single-MainHero field-battle edge in the current Host path; the receipt does not expose enough Native deployment status to conclude whether the root is party composition, Mission classification, or encounter state. A first no-model probe using `TestResults/20261111-212037` did not isolate this failure: the scripted fixture loaded, but Native pursuit did not establish an Encounter within its 60-second bound, so it stopped before entering Battle Mission; source checkpoint stayed hash-clean and no model was called. This probe is not evidence for or against the single-MainHero root cause. Host now records the Native deployment precondition fields (failed predicates, process/status, supported/reason, Mission mode, Campaign day/time mode, and deployment UI readiness) on this rejection path. `TestResults/20261111-213114` AgentHostPackage passed, covering build and packaged Host contracts; no live replay has yet verified these diagnostics. The model resumed Renown, pursued `looters_1671`, and Native presented the encounter menu with attack/surrender/leave. Dots returned 10 calls in one response, then 2 and 3 after correction, while the only currently allowed Native action was `menuSelect`. All were rejected before Native execution; the battle child ended `incidentBattleSimulation` with `provider-multiple-tool-calls`, and the game correctly remained in `MapState/encounter`. The old verified checkpoint stayed authoritative; proposed `10b46df9` had no hash and was not selected. The run used 11 parent decisions/15 requests, 4 provider failures, 37,723 prompt/7,543 completion tokens (nested loop receipt separately reports 14 decisions/25,346 prompt/4,639 completion); FreeLLMAPI returned no price receipt, so actual total cost is unknown. ## 2026-11-21：Gold路线安全取消与移动遭遇

一次 4 决策真实模型续跑在城镇 Trade 页面耗尽预算。原版 Native 快照确认页面是 Trade，但没有返回 `hasPendingChanges` 字段；旧 Host 因此未能判断是否可安全离开。现在只有在“最后一项原版操作正是城镇菜单的 Trade、且之后没有任何交易动作”时，才允许通过原版取消退回 Map；若明确报告有未提交改动、已有交易动作或入口不是 Trade，仍然拒绝取消。`BuildReceipt` 现在保留这次菜单入口，Host 回归测试覆盖缺字段路径。

验证结果：`TestResults/20261121-221956` 的 Build/AgentHostPackage 通过。真实 Native 续跑 `TestResults/20261121-222611` 用4次DeepSeek请求（估算¥0.021966048）验证了从未改动的Trade页面取消并保存回Town Map；正式源档不变。无模型回读 `TestResults/20261121-223428`确认同一原版EYC仍活动：Gold0/2000、Party1/25、Renown20/50、Companion0/1，Gold与Renown尝试暂时blocked，未有目标进展。

`TestResults/20261121-220704` 的较长12决策运行花费估算¥0.084674112、38,714输入/647输出token，先安全离开没有可售物的Trade，再沿Gold训练任务路线向`village_EW1_1`移动时被11名劫匪打断。Native显示Campaign停表、主队1人且饥饿，并给出“Fight/谈判”两个当前对话选项；决策上限在AI响应前耗尽，未发生战斗或任务进展，且没有产生可安全保存的检查点。对应筛选回执在`TestResults/20261121-220704/Temp/NativeEncounterBoundary.json`。下一次长测需让EYC/战斗子流程在同一运行预算中处理该遭遇，并保留可安全读取的Map检查点；此分支仍未完成。

## 2026-11-22 DSH direct-free provider and Campaign continuation

The local DSH XD Pool bridge is now usable as a provider-neutral OpenAI-compatible endpoint for direct free models. Kilo's provider was disabled in DSH aggregate settings even though `/v1/models` listed it; Harness preflight now requires the selected provider to be enabled/configured and the exact upstream model ID to be selected, then checks the Gateway catalog. Kilo was enabled through the local aggregate API while preserving its existing key, selected model list, and combo definitions; the non-generating provider probe accepted the configured key and listed models. The provider is still subject to upstream/free quota: its usage ledger shows `particle-ai/particle-1:free` at 7 requests, 3 failed, 1,658 prompt and 491 completion tokens across probes and one Campaign continuation. Three Campaign requests returned `502 server_error/RATE_LIMIT` without usage; no automatic fallback occurred.

OpenRouter was also disabled in the DSH aggregate. It was enabled with only the already-configured direct `particle-ai/particle-1:free` model selected; existing combo definitions and stored key were preserved. DSH `ConfiguredModelPreflight` (`TestResults/20261122-182847`) confirmed the exact model ID is in the authenticated Gateway catalog, with zero inference requests. OpenRouter's direct Particle route passed the real AgentHost JSON Schema goal-classification smoke (`TestResults/20261122-183057`): `supported-map-arrival`, 465 prompt / 91 completion tokens, 0 Native commands and no Bannerlord process. DSH usage reports one successful Particle request with those token counts. [OpenRouter lists Particle-1 Free at zero price and supports tools/structured output](https://openrouter.ai/particle-ai/particle-1%3Afree); the local DSH ledger has no currency receipt, so this is a zero-priced catalog route, not an exact billing reconciliation.

The `freshB` persistent StoryMode Campaign resumed from a hash-verified checkpoint and made 2 accepted decisions (classify EYC, then select Gold), then failed before any Native action because Kilo returned three rate-limit errors. It saved a safe same-Campaign checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261122174343-3bf57238`, SHA-256 `651518D56D9ECC9E652B30DBCA4671A77C30A3127A28C23934D8CDF7329CCCB4`; original source hash remained `6D0AD0F58DF85EA67D3D46A5F81417DE11C7DB3D21A76E57993945F0301FA8A3`, Campaign ID remains `683a2e21c4a64cfd8a1bb4f3392afc3d`, the fixture was unchanged, and process/video cleanup passed. The EYC Journal did not advance; Gold 0/2000, Party 1/25, Renown 20/50 and Companions 0/1 remain the last hash-verified values. The run used 1,106 prompt / 428 completion tokens across 5 requests (3 failures); failed-token usage was not reported, and cost/token completeness remains false.

Next continuation uses the newly verified direct OpenRouter Particle model with JSON Schema, one HTTP request per decision and a 4-decision cap from the current `freshB` checkpoint. This is the first planned Campaign run on that route; the structured provider smoke is not EYC progress. The two independent natural Campaign completions and original `Establish Your Clan` Quest completion remain the required gate.

## 2026-11-22 OpenRouter reasoning budget and next EYC continuation

A bounded real-model continuation from the same natural `freshB` StoryMode Campaign ran with the direct OpenRouter Particle-1 Free route and strict JSON Schema. It made two accepted model decisions (supported EYC classification, then select `gold`) and reached no Native action: the first objective route and subsequent Gold route each stopped on HTTP 502 `server_error/RATE_LIMIT` from Kilo in the prior run. This run instead reached OpenRouter Particle-1 and returned HTTP 200 with no structured `choices`; Host rejected it before Native. DSH's token-free usage ledger reported the OpenRouter Particle model at 2 calls, 920 prompt / 347 completion tokens across the prior classification smoke and this Campaign attempt; the Campaign's inferred delta is 455/256 tokens, while Host accepted no decision and no CNY receipt exists. The same Campaign ID `683a2e21c4a64cfd8a1bb4f3392afc3d` checkpointed safely to `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261122184502-87ae2d2a`, SHA-256 `4257D4E7EB8B32377B9F2626CD02C1A85355C6408C50A83EC21975211BE091E5`; source checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261122174343-3bf57238` hash `651518D56D9ECC9E652B30DBCA4671A77C30A3127A28C23934D8CDF7329CCCB4` was re-verified before this run, fixture remained unchanged, visual evidence and cleanup passed, and the EYC Journal did not advance.

The leading explanation is that Particle-1's default reasoning consumed the full 256-token completion budget, leaving no visible decision: OpenRouter documents that reasoning tokens count against `max_tokens` and a fully consumed reasoning budget may return no visible message. This is an inference from the 256-token DSH usage delta plus the empty-choices Host receipt, not a proven response body. Previously, `CHRONICLE_LLM_THINKING_MODE=disabled` was translated only to DeepSeek/FreeLLMAPI `thinking` syntax; the generic OpenRouter route therefore left model reasoning at its default. The Host now maps that explicit disabled setting to OpenRouter's standard `reasoning.enabled=false` for direct OpenRouter and DSH `open_router` routes, while preserving other providers' request formats. AgentHostPackage `TestResults/20261122-185951` passes fake-payload and empty-choice usage-shape regressions; Docs is required after this contract update. The next bounded continuation should re-hash this `freshB` checkpoint and rerun at most four decisions through the same OpenRouter Particle route with the reasoning-off mapping; no further model calls until the package/Docs gates pass. This remains one incomplete Campaign; the second independent Campaign and original Quest completion are still unmet. [OpenRouter reasoning parameter documentation](https://openrouter.ai/docs/guides/best-practices/reasoning-tokens).

### 2026-11-22 malformed-choice continuation and bounded diagnostics

The next persistent continuation ran as `TestResults/20261122-191100` from the reverified `freshB` source and saved a same-Campaign Native map checkpoint `ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261122191355-6a37d558`, SHA-256 `0AE1A140845D5F29383977024C229902A3B2B76CD699A3CBA7FD9CB241D6411A`; Campaign ID remains `683a2e21c4a64cfd8a1bb4f3392afc3d`. The direct DSH OpenRouter Particle route used one provider request, JSON Schema, reasoning disabled and max output 256. Host accepted zero decisions, executed zero Native actions and reported `provider-content-missing`; Host's failure receipt had no HTTP status, route or usage fields, so the upstream response shape and billing remain unverified. The run preserved the failure frame and indexed six event keyframes, removed its source video, verified the fixture and process cleanup, and left EYC incomplete.

Code inspection found a second `provider-content-missing` parser branch for one `choices` entry that lacks an object-valued `message`; unlike the existing empty-choice branch, that path did not attach safe provider diagnostics. It is a plausible explanation for the null diagnostic receipt but is **not proven** without the upstream response body, which the Harness intentionally does not retain. Contract 7.655 now records bounded HTTP status/route/shape/model/finish-reason/usage metadata for that malformed-choice branch, with a synthetic no-body regression. Before another real-model run, AgentHostPackage and Docs must pass. DSH's `/v1/models` later listed the exact direct model in a 50-row catalog; the separate local aggregate-status endpoint on port 3477 was not listening, so the explicit provider-state preflight remains unresolved and no further request is authorized by this evidence. This route has no verified currency receipt; model availability/free labels are not a billing guarantee.

The new malformed-choice regression passed in `TestResults/20261122-193351` after the first build exposed and the code corrected a local-name collision. The test made zero live model calls and issued zero Native commands. At that point the Harness status check still targeted `127.0.0.1:3477`, so the DSH provider-state preflight had not yet been proven. The later port and credential corrections below supersede that diagnostic gap; the exposed upstream credentials still need rotation before any further generation request.

### DSH status endpoint correction

The previous statement that DSH's aggregate-status endpoint was unavailable was caused by the Harness checking the wrong local port. A direct authenticated **read-only** GET to `http://127.0.0.1:2733/plugins/dsh-xdpool/aggregate` returned HTTP 200; the aggregate is enabled, `open_router` is enabled and configured, and its selected-model list contains only `particle-ai/particle-1:free` (`TestResults/20261122-194322` was a Docs run; the endpoint observation itself was zero-inference). The DSH `/v1/models` catalog is separately reachable at port 8326 and lists the exact namespaced direct model. Run-Harness contract 7.656 now uses the verified status port 2733 instead of 3477. No provider call was made while repairing this false blocker. The exposed provider credentials still need rotation before another inference request; no billed or free quota use is inferred from this status check.

The first corrected `ConfiguredModelPreflight` failed safely before inference because it inherited the user's generic `CHRONICLE_LLM_API_KEY`, which is not the DSH gateway credential: that value received HTTP 401 from `/v1/models`, while the dedicated DSH key-file value received HTTP 200 and listed the exact model. `Get-ChronicleProviderCredential` now treats `127.0.0.1:8326/v1` as a distinct provider and reads only the DSH gateway key file; it fails closed instead of borrowing a paid-provider key. A synthetic temporary-file test is included in `Test-AgentHostProvider.ps1`. The next health run then found a separate flag-forwarding bug: Run-Harness accepted `-AllowDshXdPoolFreeRoute` but omitted it when invoking the child health script. Contract 7.658 forwards that explicit opt-in; its bounded Host health receipt is `TestResults/20261122-200618` (50 catalog models, strict tool-call strategy, zero model requests and Native commands). The corrected end-to-end preflight receipts are `TestResults/20261122-200940` and `TestResults/20261122-202037` on commit `bbb90489bd9958433db742c3f6dc939b6d98cfef` (direct OpenRouter Particle ID listed, provider enabled/configured and selected, inference requests 0, Bannerlord not started, fallback disabled). Credentials for the upstream providers remain due for rotation before any generation request.

### DSH usage-ledger correlation boundary

The installed `dsh-xdpool@2.7.5` implementation reports per-call samples while its process is running; after restart it backfills usage from a 90-day day-aggregated ledger at channel/model granularity. Its backfill cannot reconstruct per-call timestamps or a Chronicle run/request ID. A read-only usage query in this continuation showed two `trae`-channel rows and no `particle-ai/particle-1:free` row, but this does not prove the earlier Chronicle request used zero tokens: the query is not correlated to that run and the response may be backfilled/retained at different granularity. Continue to use Chronicle's own response/token receipt for per-run counts, provider records only as corroboration, and report cost as unknown when the provider supplies no verifiable price.

Token-use details from the earlier `auto:noema-free` Campaign receipt `TestResults/20261122-034537`: 28 accepted decisions, 34 provider requests, 5 fallback attempts, and four distinct `custom/*` upstream models. Accepted decisions account for 76,961 prompt / 11,308 completion tokens, of which the provider reported 11,648 prompt-cache hits and 65,313 misses (15.1% hits on accepted prompt tokens). Six failed/truncated responses separately reported 20,574 prompt / 6,144 completion tokens. Total currency cost remains unknown. The evidence supports that this run's fallback route changed upstream models; it does not establish that prompt layout alone caused the cache rate. A future cache comparison must hold the model route fixed and use per-response cache fields.

The direct OpenRouter model-detail endpoint was verified without an API key or inference request: `particle-ai/particle-1:free` returns explicit prompt and completion prices of zero. Harness contract 7.659 now rechecks that exact selected OpenRouter model via the public model-detail API on every DSH configured-model preflight and rejects missing, mismatched, or nonzero price metadata before any inference. `ConfiguredModelPreflight` still reports no billing receipt; the price is a current published list-price record, not an account invoice. Other DSH providers remain blocked from Harness inference until they have their own authoritative price verifier. This establishes zero published token price for the current selected route; it does not authorize a request while the previously exposed upstream credentials await rotation.

The first dynamic pricing gate passed in `TestResults/20261122-204648`: exact model `particle-ai/particle-1:free`, prompt price `0`, completion price `0`, direct OpenRouter provider enabled/configured, exact model selected, and zero inference/game activity. This is a current published-price receipt only; the provider has not returned a bill for a Chronicle request.

OpenRouter's official Free Models documentation currently caps `:free` variants at 20 requests/minute and 50 requests/day, and says failed attempts count. Harness contract 7.660 now limits one persistent continuation to at most four provider decisions with one outer HTTP request per decision. This bounds one Chronicle run but cannot account for the same OpenRouter account's requests from other DSH clients; no daily account-headroom receipt exists. [OpenRouter Free Models and rate limits](https://openrouter.ai/collections/free-models).

#### 2026-11-23 DSH WorkBuddy 实际请求证据

用户提供的本机 DSH OpenAI 兼容网关已接入独立凭据 `CHRONICLE_DSH_XDPOOL_API_KEY`；本轮没有把该值写入仓库，也没有覆盖现有用户级 DeepSeek 地址。模型目录列出 `buddy:hy4flash`，倍率为 0.03（XD Pool 内部倍率，不是货币价格）；本机状态显示 4 个账户有正积分。Provider 将此端点固定为单请求 JSON Object，且 EYC 目标分类只向模型发送完整目标和当前允许的 goal ID；AgentHost 仍按原始 Native 请求校验结果。此投影把真实 Host 请求从 1478 字节降到 855 字节，不能单独证明模型可用。

`TestResults/20261123-001652` 的 AgentHostPackage 通过（422 Provider 契约场景、27 Campaign loop、32 BattleSimulation loop；真实模型调用 0、Native 命令 0）；`TestResults/20261123-002119` 的 Docs / OpenSpec 检查通过（126/126 系统映射、7/7 OpenSpec 检查）。真实 AgentHostProvider 在 `TestResults/20261122-234220`、`20261122-235413`、`20261123-000948`、`20261123-002417`、`20261123-002918` 均返回 HTTP 403 `risk_blocked`；最新精简请求仍相同。失败收据没有 token usage 或货币价格，故失败请求成本未知；所有尝试均 `NativeCommands=0`，没有启动游戏。

两条无游戏的简短诊断请求返回 HTTP 200，但在 64 / 48 completion-token 上限分别报告 68/64 与 93/48 tokens、`finish_reason=length`，没有形成可解析、可接受的分类决策；合计 161 prompt / 112 completion tokens，仍无货币价格收据。安装的 `dsh-xdpool` 源码仅在上游 HTTP 403 且响应码为 11140/11141 时归类 `risk_blocked`，并对该模型下的账户执行 30 分钟冷却；插件文档说明 11140 表示账号风控。Chronicle 的安全收据只保存归一化类型，没有保存上游数字码，因此该批请求的具体账号/代码无法核实。之后将同一 EYC 分类请求切到当前目录标记为倍率0的 `buddy:hy4flash-free`，`TestResults/20261123-005053`仍得到 HTTP 403 `risk_blocked`，未报告 token 用量，Native命令为0。当前两个 WorkBuddy 模型的 Host 决策都未验证成功。此前读取的汇总状态只显示全局冷却字段；后续对本地 XD Pool 状态端点进行只读核查时，4个账户均未处于账户级冷却。虽然本机插件源码类型定义含可选的`modelCooldowns`字段，实际状态响应的账号对象没有此字段，因此模型级冷却未知。该端点不关联8328请求实际选中的账户，也不显示上游原始错误码，故仍无法解释403。不要继续重发这两个路由，先从 DSH 的脱敏路由诊断确认模型/账号风险状态。

用户再次提供 `http://127.0.0.1:8328/v1` 凭据后，先读取 `/v1/models`：网关可达并列出78个模型，含`buddy:hy4flash`。Harness只读Provider健康检查`TestResults/20261123-031215`通过，采用`json_object`且0模型推理/0 Native命令。随后对该首选模型做**一次**真实EYC分类请求（最多128个输出token），收据`TestResults/20261123-031215/WorkBuddyHy4FlashSmoke/ProviderSmokeFailure.json`显示HTTP 403 `risk_blocked`，0个被接受决策/0 Native命令；安全收据不保留响应体、token usage或价格，故本次费用未知。读取目录与Provider健康成功证明本地网关/凭据/模型发现可用，不能解释上游为何风控拒绝。之后对本地XD Pool只读状态端点（`127.0.0.1:2733/plugins/dsh-xdpool/status`）核查，4个账户都未处于账户级冷却；实际状态响应没有`modelCooldowns`字段，因此模型级冷却未知。该状态不包含8328推理请求的账户关联或原始上游403码，仍不足以定位拒绝原因。8328网关在无凭据时对`/openapi.json`、`/v1/usage`和`/health`均返回401，带专用凭据后这三处均返回404；未找到可由其直接提供本次路由/用量诊断的标准端点。该结果与此前`hy4flash`/`hy4flash-free`失败一致；不重复重试，待DSH提供脱敏的上游错误码、被选账号和该模型冷却状态后再选路线。凭据只保存于用户级`CHRONICLE_DSH_XDPOOL_API_KEY`，未写入工作区；DeepSeek配置未更改。

为减少后续排障对人工查看DSH日志的依赖，Harness contract 7.668 在 Host 错误解析器中加入了受限的 WorkBuddy 错误码提取：只有匹配 DSH 的 `risk_blocked` 403 envelope，且其上游细节含白名单代码 `11140` / `11141` 时，才把该码写入安全 Provider 收据与 Campaign 进度日志；绝不保存原始消息、账号标签或响应体。Fake Provider regression 已加入 AgentHostPackage；`TestResults/20261123-034800` 包级验证通过，0真实模型调用、0 Native命令。该测试确认合成响应中的白名单代码可读且敏感标记不落盘；这不会恢复本次已丢弃响应内容，不证明后续上游错误一定带码，也不改变当前禁止重试相同路由的门禁。

另一次零推理路由核验发现两个端点不是同一路由：用户给出的 `8328/v1/models` 将`buddy:hy4flash`标为`owned_by=workbuddy`；单次403因此属于WorkBuddy路径。另一个本机DSH XD Pool端点`8326/v1/models`列出`xdpool/hy4flash`、`xdpool-global/hy4flash`及free变体；但本机XD Pool状态显示`hy4flash`和`hy4flash-free`均为`enabled=false`，且当前选择列表不含这两个模型。没有对8326发推理、没有改动模型启用设置。两种端点/路由不可互换；需先确定是修复指定的WorkBuddy通道，还是启用并测试XD Pool通道，不能因名称相近自行改路由或改变本机模型选择。

后续选择按用户最初要求的XD Pool路径推进：国内`modelSelectionCn`原先为空，因此只启用了`hy4flash-free`（零倍率、限时免费标签），保留图像模型和上下文预算设置，未启用付费`hy4flash`。写入回执为`TestResults/20261123-040341-dsh-xdpool-selection/DshPoolModelSelectionChange.json`；同次状态响应确认`enabled=true`、multiplier 0、无模型推理。此后DSH provider-ID迁移（startup.log记录`workbuddy-xdpool`→`xdpool`）使原只读插件状态路由`2733/plugins/dsh-xdpool/status`返回404；本机profile `cordis.patch.yml`仍显示CN选择只增加该免费变体，`8326/v1/models`仍列出精确模型ID。Harness 7.669增加只在状态路由失效时使用的本地profile解析+60分钟零倍率收据回退，并失败关闭。`TestResults/20261123-044039`的ConfiguredModelPreflight通过，0模型推理/0 Bannerlord；`TestResults/20261123-044417`的AgentHostPackage通过。DSH同时生成了账号同步冲突备份；当前与备份包含相同4个账号ID及相同credential快照哈希，未打印账号或凭据内容；其他账号元数据差异未分析。下一步是对所选免费route做一次最多128输出token、单HTTP请求的真实Provider分类；倍率0不是CNY账单，若上游不报告价格，费用仍标未知。

该受限冒烟在`TestResults/20261123-045007`实际发送了一条Provider请求，模型目录和本地route预检已通过，但XD Pool返回HTTP 502 `server_error` / `AUTH`，没有产生接受的决策、token usage或价格记录，Native命令为0，未启动Bannerlord。4个本地WorkBuddy凭据快照的access/refresh到期时间均未过去，但原`sourcePath`文件均不存在；这只能证明快照字段的时间戳在未来，不能证明上游凭据仍有效，也不能把`AUTH`归因为密钥、账号或模型配置中的任何一个。8328 WorkBuddy尝试仍是先前的403 `risk_blocked`，不能把两个错误合并成同一根因。当前status API已404，profile/model目录只能证实本地选择和模型暴露；不重复重试，需先恢复可用的DSH状态诊断或有效上游账户。

在两个DSH路由都未产生可用决策后，按用户此前对DeepSeek备用路由的授权，从哈希已核验的`freshB`原战役检查点做一轮**¥0.20运行上限、最多4决策、每决策1个HTTP请求、256输出token、20分钟**的真实自治续跑。官方价格页当前将`deepseek-flash`列为V4.1-Flash-1017，非高峰输入缓存未命中$0.15/M、缓存命中$0.003/M、输出$0.60/M；Harness用更高峰价换算保留。`TestResults/20261123-051034`在源检查点哈希`02D9E48B...`未变的情况下，模型2决策/2请求、2,149 prompt/115 completion tokens、估算¥0.003552096；先正确识别EYC，再选`gold`。Gold子流程在未发Native动作的情况下失败`gold-sale-no-safe-native-action`；Autonomy外层结果记录1个Native动作，检查点安全保存为`ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261123051341-23bdc17f`（SHA-256 `EEB03647...`），原EYC仍未完成。0模型检查点读取`TestResults/20261123-052704`确认同一Campaign在MapState、`Stop`、主队移动目标仍为`town_EW1`，1名主角仍有伤、Gold 0、Renown 20；进程与源存档均未改变。

源码复核定位到Gold路由缺口：子流程启动时把`moveTargetId`清空；当Native观察已有`MainParty.isMoving=true`、非空城镇目标、当前不在目标时，`BuildActionCatalog`因认为“已有移动”而过滤动作，`BuildControlCatalog`又因目标未绑定而不给`observeAgain`，于是零动作失败。Contract 7.670修复为只采用当前Native的有效 settlement target；时间正在推进时复用现有Native移动监视器被动观测最多10秒，期间不调用LLM、不改变时钟；若原生Move仍活动但`TimeControlMode=Stop`，将原状态交回父EYC循环走已经有契约的同目标Native Move恢复，不内嵌advance。遭遇、对话、抵达或目标变化都回到已有的Native/父级处理路径。`TestResults/20261123-054959` AgentHostPackage通过，合成回归覆盖当前城镇移动目标的采用与Party/到达/停队/菜单反例；Provider自检模型请求0、Native命令0。修复后的持续移动与到达后Trade还需下一轮自然Campaign续跑验证。

第二轮DeepSeek长测`TestResults/20261123-055851`从哈希校验通过的同一`freshB` Campaign（源检查点`23bdc17f`/`EEB03647...`）继续，4决策/4请求，9,544 prompt/209 completion tokens，估算¥0.020095104，低于¥0.20封顶。MainParty从`town_EW1`现存Native Move抵达Town菜单，CampaignDay 91114.948→91115.0；接着模型打开Trade、读取报价并选择Native Cancel，Gold仍0，没有交易提交。子流程因4决策预算耗尽而安全检查点到`ChronicleEycDev-freshB-20261123060145-98cebf6f`（SHA-256 `AE6CB8D039F6683D578D20F22943DA8C8D80E84B53A2257743CDC6927073918E`），原始源哈希未变、同一Campaign，游戏与Host清理通过，EYC仍未完成。此运行把先前`gold-sale-no-safe-native-action`推进为可感知的Town Trade路径，但Gold与Quest进度尚未增加；下一续跑仍须依据新Native报价离开无交易收益的Town或执行可行的原版收入行为，不能把到达Town算成Gold目标进度。

用户再次提供8328网关凭据后，2026-11-23只读`/v1/models`返回成功，共47项；其中仅列出`buddy:hy4flash-free`，没有普通`buddy:hy4flash`。没有发送推理请求。受限`ConfiguredModelPreflight`收据`TestResults/20261123-070702`在推理前因DSH WorkBuddy状态路由不可读而失败关闭；对本机2733、8328、8326及当前9565/9566端口探测`/plugins/dsh-xdpool/status`分别得到404/404/404/401/401。当前四条本地账号记录仍有未到期时间戳，但各自原始`sourcePath`都缺失；时间戳不能证明凭据有效，也无法将此前403 `risk_blocked`归因到具体上游账号。故网关凭据与模型目录已验证，本次没有模型决策或成本；不要重发已失败的WorkBuddy路线，须先恢复DSH账号/状态诊断或更换有可核验额度的路由。
